<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.100  Definitions </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal>Unless otherwise clarified by this Part, definitions set
forth in the Act also apply for purposes of this Part. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>"Act" means the Real
Estate License Act of 2000 [225 ILCS 454].</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>"Affidavit of
Non-participation" means a sworn statement made by an owner, director,
partner, manager or member of, a licensed real estate corporation, limited
liability company, partnership, limited partnership or limited liability
partnership attesting that they are either unlicensed or not actively participating
or engaging in licensed activities for that entity.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>"Board" means the Real
Estate Administration and Disciplinary Board as set forth in Section 25-10 of
the Act. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><i> </i></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'><i>"Brokerage agreement"
means an agreement between a sponsoring broker and a consumer for licensed
activities, or the performance of future licensed activities, to be provided to
a consumer in return for compensation or the right to receive compensation from
another.  Brokerage agreements may constitute either a bilateral or a
unilateral agreement between the broker and the broker's client depending upon
the content of the brokerage agreement.  All brokerage agreements shall be in
writing and may be exclusive or non-exclusive. </i>[225 ILCS 454/1-10]</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>"CE" is an abbreviation
for continuing education.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>"Credit Hour" means a
minimum of 50 minutes of instruction through a course approved by the Division in
a classroom, by interactive webinar, online distance education, correspondence or
a home study course.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>"Department" means the
Department of Financial and Professional Regulation.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:.5in'>"Designated Managing
Broker" means a managing broker who:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in'>the sponsoring broker designated
to manage its real estate brokerage, pursuant to Section 1450.700; and </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in'>has the responsibilities, and
supervises the activities, described in Section 1450.705.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'><i>"Director" means the
Director of Real Estate within the Department of Financial and Professional
Regulation.</i> [225 ILCS 454/1-10]</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>"Discipline" means a:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in'>refusal to issue or renew a
license; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in'>reprimand;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in'>probation;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in'>suspension or revocation of a
license; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in'>fine; or </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in'>any other action the Department
may deem proper.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>"Division" means the
Department of Financial and Professional Regulation‑Division of Real
Estate.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'><i>"Education Provider"
means a school licensed by the Department offering courses in pre-license,
post-license, or continuing education. </i>[225 ILCS 454/1-10]</p>

<p class=MsoNormal><i> </i></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'><i>"Electronic Signature"
means a signature in electronic form attached to or logically associated with
an electronic record.</i> [5 ILCS 175/5-105] For purposes of the Act and this
Part, any application, affidavit, or other document required to be signed may
be signed by means of an electronic signature.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>"Home Study" or "Correspondence
Course" means an education course that is administered independently by
the student in a self-study format, outside of a classroom, or physical or
virtual meeting space, which may, but is not required to, include communication
with an Illinois licensed instructor, but for which there is no requirement for
the ongoing verification of the student's participation and comprehension,
except for the administration of the proctored final examination required in
connection with a non-interactive course. Home study or correspondence courses may
include, but are not limited to, print or non-interactive media. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>"Interactive Delivery Method"
means delivery of a course, approved by the Department through a medium
allowing for interaction with the student. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>"Interactive Webinar"
means delivery of a course by a licensed instructor through a medium allowing
for live 2‑way communication between the licensed instructor and student
in which either can initiate or respond to questions, including live
instruction and real-time discussion via satellite, video or webcam that allows
for student participation and comprehension.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>"Learning Management
System" means a software application used for the administration,
documentation, tracking, reporting, and delivery of educational courses or
training programs.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>"Office" means a sponsoring
broker's place of business where the general public is invited to transact
business, or from which sponsored licensees work, where records may be
maintained, and where licenses may be readily available, as set forth in
Section 1450.730, whether or not it is the sponsoring broker's principal place
of business. All offices must meet the requirements set forth in Section
1450.610.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>"Online Distance Education"
means education courses that are interactive, but not in real time, in which
students independently learn and review material online, and, for verification
of the student's participation and comprehension, interact with an Illinois
licensed instructor or Learning Management System.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>"Post-License Education"
means the 45-hour post-license education required of new broker licensees
pursuant to Section 5-50(b) of the Act, which is comprised of three 15-hour
courses, each requiring passage of its own 50 question final examination.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>"Principal Office" means
the office location, whether physical or virtual, that serves as the principal
place of business of a sponsoring broker.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>"Proctor" means any
person, including a licensed instructor, who has a written agreement with a
licensed education provider to administer, in person or electronically,
examinations fairly and impartially. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>"Renewal Period" means,
for an initial broker license, the period 180 days prior to the expiration date
of that license.  For all other licenses, "renewal period" means the
period 90 days prior to the expiration date of the license.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>"Secretary" means the
Secretary of the Department of Financial and Professional Regulation.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>"Self-sponsor" means a licensed
managing broker who operates a real estate brokerage as a sole proprietorship.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>"Sole Owner" means a person
who has a 100% beneficial ownership interest in real estate, except as defined
in Section 10-30(c)(2) of the Act.  </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>"SOS" means the Illinois
Secretary of State.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>"Sponsoring Broker"
means a person who operates a corporation, limited liability company, partnership,
limited partnership, or limited liability partnership that is licensed by the
Department, or an individual with a managing broker license who operates as a
sole proprietor.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>"Sponsoring Broker's
Name" means the sponsoring broker's name as licensed by the Department or
the assumed name registered by the sponsoring broker with the Division, which
in the case of a franchise shall include the franchise affiliation and the name
of the individual firm.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>"Sponsorship" means that
a sponsoring broker has certified to the Division that a  broker, managing
broker, or residential leasing agent is employed by, or associated with by
written employment or independent contractor agreement, and the Division has
registered the sponsorship (see Section 5-40 of the Act).</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>"Transaction", for
purposes of this Part, exists once an agreement has been reached and an
accepted real estate contract is signed, or a lease is agreed to, by the
parties.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>"Virtual Office" means an
office from which real estate brokerage services are provided without a
dedicated office space or fixed physical location under the supervision of the
designated managing broker.  Sponsored licensees provide services within the
meaning of the term "Broker" as defined in Section 1-10 of the Act.  A
virtual office may also be a place of business for education providers who
provide services pursuant to the Act.  Virtual offices must meet the
requirements set forth in Section 1450.610 or 1450.1100.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>(Source:  Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512,
effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.110  Sponsorship </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>a)         Residential Leasing
Agent  </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         Except
for an individual working under a residential leasing agent permit (see Section
1450.240), no residential leasing agent license applicant may engage in leasing
residential real estate (see Section 1450.200) until a valid sponsorship of the
applicant has been registered with the Division by the sponsoring broker. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         A
sponsoring broker shall certify to the Division that the applicant whose name
appears on the registration is employed by or associated with that sponsoring broker.
</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         Within
24 hours after establishing the sponsorship, the sponsoring broker shall notify
the Division, in a format provided by the Division, that the residential
leasing agent is employed by, or associated with, the sponsoring broker. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>4)         A
sponsoring broker may sponsor a residential leasing agent or applicant only upon
the sponsoring broker's receipt of any of the following: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>A)        A
residential leasing agent examination pass score report stating that the applicant
passed the exam; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>B)        Termination
of sponsorship by the sponsoring broker who previously employed or was
associated with the residential leasing agent;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>C)        A
current inactive residential leasing agent license; or</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:143.7pt;text-indent:-35.4pt'>D)        A
residential leasing agent license expired for less than 2 years.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>5)         The applicant
or licensee shall, within 24 hours after establishing sponsorship, submit to
the Division, in a format provided by the Division, the following:  </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>A)        Residential
Leasing Agent Applicants </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:179.55pt;text-indent:-35.55pt'>i)          A
copy of the applicant's transcript evidencing compliance with the education
requirements, if applicable; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:179.55pt;text-indent:-35.55pt'>ii)         A
residential leasing agent examination pass score report stating that the applicant
passed the exam; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:179.55pt;text-indent:-35.55pt'>iii)        A
signed and completed residential leasing agent license application; and </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:179.55pt;text-indent:-35.55pt'>iv)        The
required residential leasing agent license application fee set forth in Section
1450.130. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>B)        Residential
Leasing Agent Licensee </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>i)          Confirmation
of sponsorship in a format provided by the Division; and </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>ii)         The
required sponsorship fee set forth in Section 1450.130.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>6)         A
sponsoring broker shall maintain a register of all sponsored residential
leasing agents and shall make the list readily available to the public (see Section
1450.730). </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><i> </i></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>b)         Broker and Managing
Broker</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         Within
24 hours after establishing the sponsorship, the sponsoring broker shall notify
the Division, in a format provided by the Division, that the broker or managing
broker is employed by, or associated with, the sponsoring broker. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         A
sponsoring broker may sponsor a broker, managing broker, or applicant only upon
the sponsoring broker's receipt of any of the following: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>A)        For
applicants, a broker or managing broker real estate examination pass score
report stating that the applicant passed the exam;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>B)        Termination
of sponsorship by the sponsoring broker who previously employed or was
associated with the broker or managing broker licensee; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>C)        A
current inactive broker or managing broker license; or</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>D)        A broker
or managing broker license expired for less than 2 years. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         The applicant
or licensee shall, within 24 hours after establishing sponsorship, submit to
the Division, in a format provided by the Division, the following:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:.5in'>A)        Broker
and Managing Broker Applicants </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>i)          A
copy of the transcript evidencing compliance with the education requirement, if
applicable; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>ii)         A
real estate examination pass score report stating that the applicant passed the
exam; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>iii)        A
signed and completed broker or managing broker application and other
documentation set forth in Sections 1450.430 and 1450.520; and </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>iv)        The
required broker or managing broker license application fee as set forth in
Section 1450.130. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:.5in'>B)        Broker
and Managing Broker Licensees </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>i)          Confirmation
of sponsorship in a format provided by the Division; and </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>ii)         The
required sponsorship fee set forth in Section 1450.130. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>4)         A
managing broker may practice as a sole proprietor.  A person or persons may form
and practice as a corporation, limited liability company, partnership, limited
partnership, or limited liability partnership provided that, prior to engaging
in licensed activities, the entity complies with the licensing requirements set
forth in Section 1450.600 and submits to the Division, in a format provided by
the Division, the following: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>A)        Confirmation
of the person serving as the designated managing broker; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>B)        The required
fee set forth in Section 1450.130;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>C)        A
completed consent to examine and audit special accounts form; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in'>D)        A signed and completed
license application. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>c)         If an applicant or
licensee fails to complete the requirements of this Section: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:.5in'>1)         The
sponsorship shall be void; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         The
applicant or licensee shall be considered to have never been authorized to
practice; and </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:.5in'>3)         The
applicant or licensee may be subject to discipline.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>d)         Within
30 days after registration of sponsorship, and receipt of the required fees and
appropriate documentation, the Division shall issue a license to the applicant
or licensee or may notify the applicant or licensee, designated managing
broker, and sponsoring broker of the basis for denial. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>e)         Licensees
shall carry a properly issued license or an electronic version of the license
and shall display the license or provide evidence of licensure upon request
when engaging in any licensed activities. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>f)         Whenever
any provision of Section 5-40 of the Act or any provision of this Part requires
that any license be surrendered, sent, obtained, delivered, or issued by or to
a licensee (including a sponsoring broker) or the Department, the manner of
doing so may include electronic transmission, including the use of any
electronic portal maintained by the Department.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>(Source: 
Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512, effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.115  Termination of Sponsorship</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>a)         Upon
termination of sponsorship by a sponsoring broker, the sponsoring broker shall
immediately notify the terminated licensee, and within 24 hours after the
termination shall notify the Division, in a format provided by the Division. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>b)         When
a licensee initiates termination with the sponsoring broker, the licensee shall,
within 24 hours of termination, notify the Division, in a format provided by
the Division, and shall immediately notify the sponsoring broker.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>c)         Once
the sponsorship has been terminated, the license is automatically changed to
inactive status and the licensee is prohibited from engaging in licensed
activities until the licensee has established a valid sponsorship with a new
sponsoring broker. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>(Source:  Amended at 45 Ill. Reg. 2851,
effective February 23, 2021)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.120  Assumed Name </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>a)         If a
sponsoring broker, acting as a sole proprietor, operates under any name other
than that appearing on the sponsoring broker's license, the sponsoring broker
shall submit to the Division a copy of the sponsoring broker's registration
under the Assumed Business Name Act [805 ILCS 405].</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>b)         If a
sponsoring broker, acting as a partnership, corporation, limited liability
corporation, limited partnership, or limited liability partnership, operates
under any name other than that appearing on the sponsoring broker's license,
the sponsoring broker shall submit to the Division a copy of the filing or
certificate authorizing it to do business under an assumed name from the
Illinois Secretary of State and in accordance with the Assumed Business Name
Act [805 ILCS 405].</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>c)         Sponsored
licensees, including a team or teams, may not operate under an assumed business
name other than an assumed business name of their sponsoring broker. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>d)         The sponsoring
broker shall submit the information to the Division within 30 days after registration
of the assumed name in a format provided by the Division. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>(Source: 
Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512, effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.130  Fees </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>a)         Residential
Leasing Agent License and Residential Leasing Agent Student </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         The
application fee for an initial residential leasing agent license is $100. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         The renewal
fee for an unexpired residential leasing agent license is $150 per renewal. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         The
late fee for a residential leasing agent license expired for no more than 2
years is $75. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>4)         The application
fee for a residential leasing agent permit is $50. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>5)         The
restoration fee for a residential leasing agent license expired for more than 2
years but less than 5 years is the sum of all lapsed renewal fees and a late fee.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>6)         A
person receiving an initial license during the first renewal period shall not
be required to pay the initial renewal fee and will be issued a license
expiring on the second renewal deadline.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>b)         Broker License</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         The application
fee for an initial broker license is $150. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         The renewal
fee for an unexpired broker license is $200 per renewal. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         The late
fee for a broker license expired for no more than 2 years is $75. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>4)         The
restoration fee for a broker license expired for more than 2 years but less
than 5 years is the sum of all lapsed renewal fees and a late fee.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>5)         The
fee to transfer from a managing broker license to a broker license is $150.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>6)         A
person receiving an initial license within the first renewal period shall not
be required to pay the initial renewal fee and will be issued a license
expiring on the second renewal deadline.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>c)         Managing Broker License </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:108.3pt;text-indent:-36.3pt'>1)         The
application fee for an initial managing broker license is $175. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:108.3pt;text-indent:-36.3pt'>2)         The
renewal fee for an unexpired managing broker license is $250 per renewal.  </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         The late
fee for a managing broker license expired for no more than 2 years is $75. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>4)         The
restoration fee for a managing broker license expired for more than 2 years but
less than 5 years is the sum of all lapsed renewal fees and a late fee.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>5)         A
person receiving an initial license during the first renewal period shall not
be required to pay the initial renewal fee and will be issued a license
expiring on the second renewal deadline.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>6)         The
application fee for an initial virtual office license for a self-sponsored
managing broker under this subsection shall be $200 and in addition to the
initial application fee for the managing broker license.  The renewal fee for a
virtual office license shall be $100 and also in addition to the renewal fee
for the managing broker license.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>d)         Real Estate Auction
Certification</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:.5in'>1)         The
application fee for an initial real estate auction certification is $125. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         The
renewal fee for an unexpired real estate auction certification is $300 per renewal.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         The
late fee for a real estate auction certification expired for no more than 2
years is $75.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>4)         A
person receiving an initial certificate during the first renewal period shall
not be required to pay the initial renewal fee and will be issued a certificate
expiring on the second renewal deadline.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>e)         Corporation,
Limited Liability Company, Partnership, Limited Partnership, or Limited
Liability Partnership License </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:106.65pt;text-indent:-34.65pt'>1)        The
application fee for an initial corporation, limited liability company,
partnership, limited partnership, or limited liability partnership license is $250.
</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:106.65pt;text-indent:-34.65pt'>2)        The
renewal fee for an unexpired corporation, limited liability company,
partnership, limited partnership, or limited liability partnership license is $300
per renewal. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:106.65pt;text-indent:-34.65pt'>3)        The
late fee for a corporation, limited liability company, partnership, limited
partnership, or limited liability partnership license expired for no more than
2 years is $75. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:106.65pt;text-indent:-34.65pt'>4)        An
entity receiving its initial license during the first renewal period shall not
be required to pay the initial renewal fee and will be issued a license
expiring on the second renewal deadline.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:106.65pt;text-indent:-34.65pt'>5)        The
application fee for an initial virtual office license for an entity under this
subsection shall be $200 and in addition to the initial application fee for the
entity license.  The renewal fee for a virtual office license shall be $100 and
also in addition to the renewal fee for the entity license.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>f)         Education Provider, Pre-license
Instructor and Course License Fees </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         The application
fee for an initial education provider license is $1,050. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         The renewal
fee for an unexpired education provider license is $1,100 per renewal. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         The late
fee for an education provider license expired for no more than 2 years is $75. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>4)         The
application fee for an initial pre-license instructor license is $150.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>5)         The
renewal fee for an unexpired pre-license instructor license is $300 per renewal.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>6)         The
late fee for a pre-license instructor license expired for no more than 2 years
is $75. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>7)         The
application fee for an initial pre-license course license is $150. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>8)         The
application fee for a revised format pre-license course license is $150.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>9)         The
renewal fee for an unexpired pre-license course license is $150 per renewal.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-40.5pt'>10)        The
late fee for a pre-license course license expired for no more than 2 years is $75.
 </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-40.5pt'>11)        The
application fee for an initial post-license course license is $150.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-40.5pt'>12)        The
application fee for a revised format post-license course license is $150.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-40.5pt'>13)        The
renewal fee for an unexpired post-license course license is $150 per renewal.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-40.5pt'>14)        The
late fee for a post-license course license expired for no more than 2 years is $75.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-40.5pt'>15)        An
education provider, pre-license instructor, pre-license course, or post-license
course receiving an initial license during the renewal period shall not be
required to pay the initial renewal fee and will be issued a license expiring
on the second renewal deadline.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-40.5pt'>16)        The
application fee for an initial virtual office license for an education provider
under this subsection shall be $200 and in addition to the initial application
fee for the education provider license.  The renewal fee for a virtual office
application shall be $100 and also in addition to the renewal fee for the
education provider license.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>g)         Continuing Education,
Instructor and Course License Fees </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         The
application fee for an initial CE instructor license is $100. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         The
renewal fee for an unexpired CE instructor license is $200 per renewal.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         The
late fee for a CE instructor license expired for no more than 2 years is $75.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:108.3pt;text-indent:-36.3pt'>4)         The
application fee for an initial CE course license is $150. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:108.3pt;text-indent:-36.3pt'>5)         The
application fee for a revised format CE course license is $150.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:108.3pt;text-indent:-36.3pt'>6)         The
renewal fee for an unexpired CE course license is $150 per renewal.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:108.3pt;text-indent:-36.3pt'>7)         The
late fee for a CE course license expired for no more than 2 years is $75.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:108.3pt;text-indent:-36.3pt'>8)         A
CE instructor or CE course receiving an initial license during the renewal
period shall not be required to pay the initial renewal fee and will be issued
a license expiring on the second renewal deadline.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>h)         General </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:108.3pt;text-indent:-36.3pt'>1)         All
fees paid pursuant to the Act and this Section shall be made payable to the
Department of Financial and Professional Regulation and are non-refundable. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:108.3pt;text-indent:-36.3pt'>2)         The
fee for a certification of a licensee's record for any purpose is $35. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:108.3pt;text-indent:-36.3pt'>3)         Applicants
for an examination as a residential leasing agent, broker, managing broker, instructor
or real estate auction certification holder shall be required to pay the cost
of taking the examination.  If a designated testing service is utilized for the
examination, the fee shall be paid directly to the designated testing service. 
Failure to appear for the examination on the scheduled date, at the time and
place specified, after the applicant's application for examination has been
received and acknowledged, shall result in the forfeiture of the examination
fee. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><span style='color:black'> </span></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:108.3pt;text-indent:-36.3pt'><span
style='color:black'>4)         The fee for requesting credit for CE obtained
while out-of-state (see Section 5-75 of the Act) is</span> $50 per course. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:108.3pt;text-indent:-36.3pt'>5)         The
fee for processing a sponsorship transfer is $35. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:108.3pt;text-indent:-36.3pt'>6)         The
fee for a copy of a transcript of the proceedings under Section 20-62 of the
Act is the cost of a copy of the transcript.  A copy of the balance of the
record will be provided at the Division's cost of producing the record. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:108.3pt;text-indent:-36.3pt'>7)         The
fee for certifying the record referred to in Section 20-73 of the Act is $1 per
page.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:108.3pt;text-indent:-36.3pt'>8)         Each
university, college, community college, or school that is a public institution
of higher education supported by public funds, and located in the State of
Illinois shall be exempt from the education provider and course licensure fees,
provided that the institution meets the following criteria and certifies to the
Division that: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>A)        The
facility is domiciled in the State of Illinois and supported by public funds;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>B)        The
instructors are approved and licensed by the Department;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>C)        The courses
are approved and licensed by the Department; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>D)        The pre-license
or CE program is not independently established as a for-profit division of the
university, college, community college, or school.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>(Source:  Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512,
effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.140  Renewals</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>a)         Every residential leasing agent license expires on July 31 of
each even-numbered year. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>b)         Every broker license expires on April 30 of each even‑numbered
year. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>c)         Every managing broker license expires on April 30 of each odd-numbered
year. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>d)         Sponsoring brokers shall also submit a properly completed
consent to examine and audit special accounts form required by the Division.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>e)         Every
real estate auction certification expires on <span style='color:black'>December
31 of each even-numbered year. A real estate auction certificate holder whose certificate
has been expired for more than 2 years shall be required to meet the
requirements for a new certificate.</span></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>f)         Every corporation, limited liability company, partnership,
limited partnership, and limited liability partnership license expires on
October 31 of each even-numbered year. Each renewal, the licensee shall submit
the following: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>1)         A properly completed consent to examine and an audit special
accounts form; and </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>2)         Notice of any change of business information as provided for
in Section 1450.150, if applicable. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>g)         Renewal applications shall be submitted in a format provided
by the Division.  All renewals, if the licensee is seeking an active license,
shall include the name and license number of the sponsoring broker.  Failure to
receive a renewal notice shall not constitute a valid reason for failure to
renew a license. No license shall be renewed until the required fee is paid.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>h)         Practicing or offering to practice on an expired or inactive
license shall constitute unlicensed practice and shall be grounds for
discipline pursuant to Section 20-20 of the Act. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>i)          Any licensee whose license has expired for more than 2 years
shall not be eligible for renewal of that license.  Any licensee whose license
has been expired for less than 2 years may renew the license at any time by
complying with the requirements of this Part, by paying the fees required by
Section 1450.130 and providing satisfactory evidence of completion of the CE
required by this Part.  (See Section 5-50 of the Act.)</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>j)          Any residential leasing agent, broker or managing broker
whose license under the Act has expired is eligible to renew, reinstate, or
restore without paying any lapsed fees, and without completing the continuing
education requirements for that licensure period, provided that the license expired
while the licensee was: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>1)         On active duty with the United States Army, United States
Navy, United States Marine Corps, United States Air Force, United States Coast
Guard, or State Militia called into the service or training for the United
States; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>2)         Engaged in training or education under the supervision of the
United States prior to induction into military service; or </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>3)         Serving as an employee of the Department.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'><span style='color:black'>4)         A licensee qualifying under this
subsection (j) may renew the license within a period of 2 years following the
termination of service and is not required to take an examination;</span></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><span style='color:black'> </span></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>5)         An individual qualifying under this subsection (j) shall be
issued a license equivalent to the license held when employed by the Department
or the license held immediately prior to their military service.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>6)         A licensee qualifying under this subsection (j) must furnish
the Division with satisfactory evidence of service, training, or education, and
termination under honorable conditions within 2 years after the termination of
service.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>k)         As set forth in Section 5-50(g) of the Act, the Division, upon
request, shall prepare and deliver by mail or electronically to the sponsoring
broker a listing of licensees who, according to the Division, are sponsored by
that sponsoring broker. The sponsoring broker shall notify the Division
concerning any inaccuracies in the listing within 5 business days after its
receipt.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>l)          A notice of renewal shall be sent to all sponsoring brokers,
by mail or electronically, for each of the sponsored licensees of a sponsoring
broker.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>m)        Licensees
engaging in licensed activities in the form of a corporation, limited liability
company, partnership, limited partnership or limited liability partnership
must:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         Remain
in active status and good standing in accordance with the requirements of the
jurisdiction where it is registered and must be authorized to conduct or transact
business; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         Notify
the Division, in a format provided by the Division, within 48 hours of any
change to its status/registration resulting in the inability to conduct or transact
business in the jurisdiction in which it is registered/was authorized to conduct
or transact business; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         Be
authorized to conduct or transact business in Illinois.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in'>(Source:  Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512,
effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.145  Restoration</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>a)         A
licensee may petition for restoration of a license at any time after the
successful completion of a disciplinary term; or after a license has been
inactive or expired for more than 2 years.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>b)         A
person applying for restoration of a residential leasing agent license expired
for more than 2 years but less than 5 years shall submit the following: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         A
signed and completed residential leasing agent restoration application, in a format
provided by the Division; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         Proof
of completion of the CE requirements set forth in Section 1450.250 for all license
terms that occurred prior to the date of the restoration application; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:.5in'>3)         The
required fee set forth in Section 1450.130.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>c)         A
person applying for restoration of a broker license expired for more than 2
years but less than 5 years shall submit the following: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         A
signed and completed broker restoration application, in a format provided by
the Division; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         Proof
of completion of the CE requirements set forth in Section 1450.450 for all license
terms that occurred prior to the date of restoration application; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         If
applicable, proof of completion of the 45-hour post-license education if upon
restoration the first renewal term is part of the elapsed period prior to the
date of restoration application; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:.5in'>4)         The
required fee set forth in Section 1450.130.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>d)         A
person applying for restoration of a managing broker license expired for more
than 2 years but less than 5 years shall submit the following: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         A
signed and completed managing broker restoration application, in a format
provided by the Division; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         Proof
of completion of the CE requirements set forth in Section 1450.540 for all license
terms that occurred prior to the date of the restoration application; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:.5in'>3)         The
required fee set forth in Section 1450.130.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>e)         A
person with a license expired for more than 5 years shall comply with the fees
and requirements as a new applicant.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>f)         An
individual who seeks restoration of their license that expired while they were
either on active duty in the United States military or in military training, or
during employment with the Department, shall fulfill the requirements under
Section 1450.140(j).</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>g)         A
person may apply for restoration of a suspended, revoked, or license on
probation at any time after the successful completion of a term of suspension,
revocation, or probation of a license by filing a Petition to Restore in a
format provided by the Department pursuant to its Rules of Practice in
Administrative Hearings (68 Ill. Adm. Code 1110.30).  The Department may
restore the license in the manner provided in Section 20-69 of the Act and the
requirements in this Section.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>(Source:  Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512,
effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.150  Required</b><b> Information of
Applicant or Licensee </b> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>a)         Address
of Record and Email Address of Record </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         Pursuant
to Section 2105-7 of the Civil Administrative Code of Illinois [20 ILCS 2105],
all applicants and licensees shall provide to the Division a valid address,
which shall not be a post office box, and a valid email address, which shall
serve as the address of record and email address of record, respectively, at
the time of application for licensure or renewal of a license; and </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         Pursuant
to Section 5-41 an applicant or licensee shall inform the Division, in a format
provided by the Division, of any change of address, email address, phone, or
office location within 24 hours after such change.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         The
Division may provide service of documents by email to an applicant's or
licensee's email address of record in accordance with the Act, the Department
of Professional Regulation Law [20 ILCS 2105], or Section 10-75 of the Illinois
Administrative Procedure Act [5 ILCS 100].  Service by email shall be deemed
complete on the day of transmission regardless of whether the documents are
delivered to the applicant's or licensee's spam or junk email folder.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>b)         Aliases,
Diminutives, and Nicknames. Within 14 days after the change, each licensee shall
notify the Division, in a format provided by the Division, of any name change. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         The
licensee shall notify the Division if the licensee regularly practices under an
alias, diminutive, or nickname of the licensee's name.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         If an
individual licensee changes names by court order or due to marital status
change, the licensee shall:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>A)        Notify
the Division of the name change; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>B)        Provide
the Division a copy of the marriage certificate or portions of the court order
relating to the name change; and </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>C)        Indicate
the name to be utilized for licensure.  </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         The
licensee shall ensure that all CE certificates are issued under the name of
licensure. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>c)         Office
Location.  Each licensee shall provide the Division, in a format provided by
the Division, all physical office locations, virtual office websites or digital
platforms.  Each licensee shall notify the Division, in a format provided by
the Division, of a change in any physical office location or virtual office
website or digital platform, within 24 hours after the change in location.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>d)         Each
sponsoring broker shall, within 24 hours, notify the Division, in a format
provided by the Division, of any change of business information. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         When
a licensee acquires or transfers any interest in a licensed corporation,
limited liability company, partnership, limited partnership, or limited
liability partnership licensed under the Act. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         When
a licensee becomes an officer, manager, member or partner of a corporation,
limited liability company, partnership, limited partnership, or limited
liability partnership, including any associated changes in designated managing
brokers or office location. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         When
the licensee operates under an assumed name, it shall comply with the
applicable provisions of Section 1450.120.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>(Source:  Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512,
effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.160  Dual Licensure</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>a)         A licensee may not
simultaneously hold licenses as a:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:.5in'>1)         Managing
broker and residential leasing agent;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:.5in'>2)         Managing
broker and broker; or</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:.5in'>3)         Broker
and residential leasing agent.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>b)         Upon
the issuance of a new license, the prior license shall be cancelled and no
longer active unless there is a request to transfer pursuant to Section
1450.550. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>(Source:  Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512,
effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.170  Exemption Under Section 5-20(1) of the
Act (Repealed)</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>(Source:  Repealed at 41 Ill. Reg. 12561,
effective October 6, 2017)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>
<br><p class="subheading">SUBPART B:  LEASING AGENT LICENSING AND EDUCATION</p>

<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b> </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.200  Residential
Leasing Agent General Provisions</b> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>a)         A licensed residential leasing agent shall not engage in any
licensed activities other than licensed activities related to leasing of
residential real property. A licensed residential leasing agent may not engage
in any licensed activities described in Section 1-10 of the Act that do not
relate to leasing residential real property or would otherwise require a broker
license. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>b)         No person other than a licensed managing broker, broker or residential
leasing agent or a person working under a residential leasing agent permit
shall engage in leasing residential real property for another and for compensation.
 </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>c)         No residential leasing agent licensee may accept compensation
for the performance of leasing residential real property except from the
sponsoring broker by whom the licensee is sponsored.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>(Source:  Amended at 45 Ill. Reg. 2851,
effective February 23, 2021)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.205  Residential Leasing Agent Pre-License
Education Requirements</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal>An applicant for a residential leasing agent license must
complete 15 credit hours of instruction recommended by the Board and approved
by the Division, as set forth in Section 5-10(a)(5) of the Act. All education providers,
pre-license instructors, and pre-license courses relating to residential leasing
agent licensure must be approved by the Division and licensed pursuant to
Subpart K.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>(Source:  Amended at 45 Ill. Reg. 2851,
effective February 23, 2021)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.210  Residential Leasing Agent Examination </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>a)         Each
applicant for a residential leasing agent license shall submit an application
for examination as determined by the Division or its designated testing service,
including: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>1)         Certification that the
applicant is at least 18 years of age;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         Successful
completion of high school or equivalency required under Section 5-10 of the Act;
</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         The
required fee set forth in Section 1450.130; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>4)         Proof
of completion of 15 hours of instruction in real estate courses recommended by
the Board and approved (see Section 1450.205).</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>b)         Approved
pre-license education shall be valid for a period of 2 years after the date of satisfactory
completion of all required pre-license education (see Section 5-35 of the Act).
</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>c)         If a
person fails the examination four consecutive times, that person must repeat
the pre-license education requirement prior to taking the examination again,
and the applicant must complete a new application for examination.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>(Source:  Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512,
effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.220  Application for Residential Leasing
Agent License by Examination</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>a)         Each applicant for a residential
leasing agent license shall submit to the Division:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         A
signed and completed application, in a format provided by the Division;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:.5in'>2)         The
required fee set forth in Section 1450.130;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         Proof
of successful completion of the examination authorized by the Division;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>4)         Proof
of sponsorship if the applicant is seeking an active license (see Section
1450.110); and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:.5in'>5)         A
transcript, if applicable.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>b)         Within
30 days after receipt and approval of required fees and other documentation,
the Division shall issue a residential leasing agent license to the licensee or
notify the applicant of the basis for denial. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>c)         An
applicant shall have one year from the date of receipt of a passing examination
score to submit an application to the Division and to meet the requirements for
licensure.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>(Source: 
Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512, effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.230  Leasing Agent Termination of Employment
(Repealed)</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>(Source: 
Repealed at 40 Ill. Reg. 12588, effective September 2, 2016)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b> </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.240  Residential
Leasing Agent Permit</b> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>a)         Pursuant to Section 5-5(d) of the Act, a person engaging in leasing
residential real estate may engage in residential leasing activities for a
period of 120 consecutive days without being licensed if they first obtain a residential
leasing agent permit, which shall be valid for a period of 120 days.  A permit
holder shall comply with all provisions of the Act and this Subpart as if the
permit holder were a residential leasing agent licensee and shall be subject to
standards of practice and disciplinary provisions as if the permit holder were
a residential leasing agent licensee. A sponsoring broker for a permit holder
shall be responsible for the activities and actions of a permit holder as if
the permit holder were a residential leasing agent licensee. A permit holder
may only be sponsored by one sponsoring broker during one 120 consecutive
calendar day period. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>b)         Within 24 hours after employing, or associating with, by a
written independent contract agreement with, a permit holder seeking a
residential leasing agent license, a sponsoring broker shall submit the
following information to the Division, in a format provided by the Division: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>1)         The name, address and other information requested by the
Division to identify the permit holder and sponsoring broker; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>2)         Certification by the permit holder and the sponsoring broker
or designated managing broker that the permit holder:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>A)        Will not work for more than 120 consecutive calendar days without
having been issued a residential leasing agent license;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>B)        Is pursuing licensure under Section 5-5(d) of the Act; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>C)        Is at least 18 years of age; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>D)        Has successfully completed a four-year course of study in a
high school or secondary school or an equivalent course of study; and </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>E)        Is at the time of association, or will be within a period of 60
days, enrolled in a residential leasing agent course of instruction approved by
the Division. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>c)         Upon expiration of the 120 consecutive calendar day period,
the permit holder shall immediately cease engaging in leasing residential real
estate unless the person has been issued a residential leasing agent license
and has valid sponsorship. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>d)         A person shall not practice under a residential leasing agent
permit more than one time. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-autospace:none'>(Source: 
Amended at 45 Ill. Reg. 2851, effective February 23, 2021)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b> </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.250  Residential
Leasing Agent</b> <b>Continuing Education </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>a)         Each residential leasing agent licensee shall complete, during
the current term of the license, a minimum of 8 CE hours from a single core
curriculum recommended by the Board and approved by the Division. Approved
courses shall, at a minimum, cover recent changes in the Act and other laws
affecting the leasing of residential real estate, including fair housing and
human rights issues related to residential leasing, advertising and marketing,
leases, applications, credit reports and criminal history, handling of funds,
ownership-tenant relationships, owner-tenant laws, environmental issues related
to residential real estate, and sexual harassment prevention training (see
Section 2105-15.5 of the Department of Professional Regulation Law [20 ILCS
2105]). </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>b)         A licensee is not required to complete CE requirements for the
first renewal following the issuance of the initial residential leasing agent
license. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>c)         Education providers, instructors and courses must be approved
by the Division as set forth in Sections 1450.1100, 1450.1105, 1450.1145 and
1450.1155. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>d)         Certification of Compliance with CE Requirements </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>1)         Each licensee shall certify, on the renewal application, full
compliance with the CE requirements of this Section. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>2)         The Division may, in the context of compliance audits, require
additional evidence demonstrating compliance with the CE requirements.  Each
licensee shall retain proof of completed CE. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>3)         In the context of a compliance audit, the Division shall
accept verification submitted directly from a licensed education provider on
behalf of a licensee as proof of CE compliance. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>4)         Failure to comply with CE requirements is a violation of the
Act and may subject a licensee to a citation or such other discipline as set
forth in the Act and this Part. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>e)         The Division shall conduct audits to verify compliance with
this Section in accordance with Section 1450.960. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>(Source:  Amended at 45 Ill. Reg. 2851, effective February 23, 2021)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b> </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.260  Approved
Courses, Schools and Instructors for Leasing Agents</b> <b>(Repealed)</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-autospace:none'>(Source:  Repealed
at 40 Ill. Reg. 12588, effective September 2, 2016)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>
<br><p class="subheading">SUBPART C:  LICENSING AND EDUCATION FOR SALESPERSON</p>

<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b> </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.300  Salesperson
Educational Requirements to Obtain a License</b> <b>(Repealed)</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b> </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-autospace:none'>(Source: 
Repealed at 40 Ill. Reg. 12588, effective September 2, 2016)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal><b> </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.310  Salesperson Examination (Repealed)</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in'>(Source:  Repealed at 40 Ill. Reg. 12588,
effective September 2, 2016)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b> </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.320 
Applications for Salesperson License by Examination</b> <b>(Repealed)</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-autospace:none'>(Source: 
Repealed at 40 Ill. Reg. 12588, effective September 2, 2016)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b> </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.330  Application
for Salesperson License by Reciprocity (Repealed)</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-autospace:none'>(Source: 
Repealed at 40 Ill. Reg. 12588, effective September 2, 2016)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b> </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.340  Salesperson
Continuing Education</b><b> (Repealed)</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-autospace:none'>(Source:
