Repealed at 40 Ill. Reg. 12588, effective September 2, 2016)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>
<br><p class="subheading">SUBPART D:  BROKER LICENSING AND EDUCATION</p>

<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.400  Broker Pre-License
Education Requirements</b> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'>An applicant for a broker
license must complete 75 credit hours of instruction (see Section 1450.1105). 
The pre-license education shall be valid for 2 years after the satisfactory
completion of all required pre-license education.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in'>(Source:  Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512,
effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b> </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.410  Broker
Post-License Education Requirements </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b><i> </i></b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>a)         Brokers shall be required to complete 45 hours of post-license
education consisting of three 15-hour courses covering applied brokerage
principles, risk management/discipline, and transactional issues, as required
by the Act. This education must address the practical application of
pre-license topics to the practice of real estate (see Section 1450.1105(c)).
Brokers shall also pass an education provider administered 50-question examination
for each course. The education and examinations shall be completed prior to the
first renewal of the license.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>b)         Brokers taking the 45-hour post-license education during their
first license term shall not be required to complete any other CE during that license
term.  The 45-hour post-license education must be completed within the 2 years
immediately preceding the broker's first renewal.  However, those individuals
who received their initial broker license within 180 days preceding the first
broker renewal deadline must complete the 45-hour post-license education and
pass each of the three 15-hour course final exams prior to the second broker
renewal deadline.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>c)         The Division shall conduct audits to verify compliance with
this Section.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>d)         The requirements of this Section do not apply to attorneys
currently admitted to practice law by the Supreme Court of Illinois.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-autospace:none'>(Source: 
Amended at 45 Ill. Reg. 2851, effective February 23, 2021)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.420  Broker
Examination</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>a)         Each applicant for a broker license shall submit an
application for examination as determined by the Division or its designated
testing service, including: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>1)         Certification that the applicant is at least 18 years of age; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>2)         Successful completion of high school or equivalency required
under Section 5-27 of the Act; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>3)         The required fee set forth in Section 1450.130; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>4)         Proof of one of the following: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>A)        Currently admitted to practice law by the Supreme Court of
Illinois; or</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>B)        Completion of 75 hours of instruction in real estate courses recommended
by the Board and approved by the Division (see Section 1450.1105).</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>b)         If a person fails the examination four consecutive times, the
person must repeat the pre-license education requirement set forth in Section
5-27(a)(5) of the Act prior to taking the examination again, in addition to the
other requirements enumerated generally in Section 5-27 and must complete a new
application for examination. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>c)         As set forth in Section 5-35 of the Act, the 2- year time
period after the completion of pre-license education during which that
education will be accepted applies to approved pre-license education. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-autospace:none'>(Source: 
Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512, effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.430  Application
for Broker License by Examination</b> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>a)         Each applicant for a broker license shall submit to the
Division: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>1)         A signed and completed application in a format provided by the
Division; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>2)         The required fee set forth in Section 1450.130; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>3)         Proof of successful completion of the examination authorized
by the Division; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>4)         Proof of sponsorship if the applicant is seeking an active
license (see Section 1450.110); and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>5)         A transcript, if applicable. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>b)         An applicant shall have one year from the date of receipt of a
passing examination score to submit an application to the Division and to meet
the requirements for licensure. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>c)         If an applicant for a broker license currently holds a residential
leasing agent license, the residential leasing agent license must be in good
standing.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-autospace:none'>(Source: 
Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512, effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.440  Application
for Broker License by Reciprocity</b> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>a)         Until December 31, 2025, an Illinois broker license shall be
issued by reciprocity to a broker under the following conditions: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>1)         The broker holds an active broker license, or its equivalent,
by examination in a state with a reciprocal agreement with the Department; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>2)         The broker licensing standards of that state are substantially
equivalent to or greater than the minimum standards in Illinois; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>3)         The broker maintains a physical or virtual place of business,
as permitted by law, in the broker's state of licensure; and </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>4)         The broker submits:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>A)        An application, in a format provided by the Division;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>B)        The required fee set forth in Section 1450.130; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>C)        A certification, or a statement bearing the seal of the
licensing authority, from each state of licensure, evidencing: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>i)          An active broker license in that state; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>ii)         That the license is in good standing; and </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>iii)        Any disciplinary action taken against the broker; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>D)        Proof of passing an examination approved by the Division on
Illinois specific real estate laws; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>E)        If the broker does not maintain a definite place of business
within the State of Illinois, a written statement that: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>i)          appoints the Secretary to act as the broker's agent upon whom
all judicial and other process may be served; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>ii)         acknowledges and agrees to abide by all of the provisions of
the Act and this Part with respect to all of the broker's licensed activities
within and relating to the State of Illinois; and </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>iii)        assents to jurisdiction of the Department; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>F)         Proof of sponsorship if the applicant is seeking an active
license. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>b)         Any licensee who renews a license issued under a reciprocal
agreement assents to jurisdiction of the Department without regard to the
location of the licensee's domicile or principal place of business or office
locations. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>c)         All requirements for licensure by reciprocity shall be met
within one year after the date of original application, but no later than
December 31, 2025, or the application shall be denied and the fee forfeited;
and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>d)         No application for licensure as a broker pursuant to a
reciprocal agreement and this Section shall be submitted or granted on or after
January 1, 2026, regardless of whether the application is new or if it seeks
reconsideration of a prior application.  Licenses granted under reciprocal
agreements prior to January 1, 2026, shall remain in force and may be renewed
in the same manner as provided for a broker license in the Act and this Part. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-autospace:none'>(Source: 
Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512, effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.450  Broker
Continuing Education</b> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>a)         Continuing Education Hour Requirements </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>1)         As set forth in Section 5-70 of the Act, each broker, except
as otherwise provided in Section 5-70(b), is required to complete 12 CE hours during
the current term of the license. The total of 12 CE hours of courses approved
by the Division may be taken at any time during the current term of the license.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>2)         As set forth in Section 5-50(b) of the Act, brokers receiving
their initial broker license are required to complete the 45-hour post-license
education during their first license term and are not required to complete any
other CE during that license term.  If an individual receives the initial
broker license within 180 days preceding the first broker renewal deadline, the
broker must complete the 45-hour post-license education prior to the second
broker renewal deadline. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>3)         Brokers licensed in Illinois but residing and practicing in
other states shall comply with the CE requirements set forth in this Section,
unless they are exempt (see Section 5-70(b) of the Act).</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>4)         The Division shall conduct audits to verify compliance with
this Section. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>b)         Approved Continuing Education </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>1)         CE credit may be earned for verified attendance at,
participation in, or completion of a licensed course offered by a licensed education
provider (see Sections 1450.1100 and 1450.1105). </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>2)         CE credit may be earned by passing a proctored exam for a
correspondence or home study course offered and verified by a licensed Education
Provider (see Section 1450.1155). </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>3)         As set forth in Section 5-70 of the Act, CE credit shall be
obtained through completion of a single core curriculum and an elective
curriculum recommended by the Board and approved by the Division. CE
requirements shall be deemed satisfied upon the successful completion of the
following: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>A)        Core Curriculum. A minimum of 6 CE credit hours per two year license
term in a core curriculum course recommended by the Board and approved by the Division,
which must include at least 2 credit hours of fair housing training.  Pursuant
to Section 5-70, the core curriculum may also cover subjects including, but not
limited to, advertising, agency, agency disclosures, property disclosures,
residential leasing agent management, escrow, fair housing, and license law.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>B)        Elective Curriculum. No fewer than 6 CE credit hours per 2-year
license term in elective curriculum courses approved by the Division, which may
cover subjects including, but not limited to, license law, escrow, antitrust,
fair housing, agency, appraisal, property management, residential brokerage, farm
property management, transaction management rights and duties of parties in a
transaction, commercial brokerage and leasing, real estate financing,
disclosures, residential leasing agent management, advertising, broker
supervision and designated managing broker responsibility, professional
conduct, diversity equity and inclusion, use of technology, and any training
required by law (e.g., sexual harassment prevention training (see 20 ILCS
2105/2105-15.5)) may be credited toward elective hours. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>4)         As set forth in Section 5-70(g) of the Act, CE credit may be
earned by a licensed pre-license or CE instructor for teaching a licensed CE
course. Credit for teaching a licensed CE course may only be earned once for
that course during a license term. One hour of instruction is equal to one CE hour.
</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>5)         As set forth in Section 5-75 of the Act, if licensees earn CE credit
hours in another state or territory for which they will claim credit toward
compliance in Illinois, the licensee shall submit an application and a $50 fee,
within 90 days after completion of the CE course and prior to expiration of the
license. The Division will review and may approve the course if the education
provider, course, and delivery method of assessing participation and comprehension
are substantially equivalent to those licensed in Illinois. In determining
whether the education provider and course are substantially equivalent, the Division
will use the criteria in Sections 5-70 through 5-80 of the Act and this
Section. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>6)         CE credit will only be given for CE courses offered by
education providers licensed by the Division. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>7)         Pursuant to Section 5-70(j) of the Act, no more than 12 hours
of CE credit may be taken in one calendar day.  If a proctored final exam is
required for a licensee to receive credit for the CE course or courses, the
exam may be given either at the end of each individual course or group of
courses.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>8)         CE credit shall not be given for pre-license and post-license
education courses, except as set forth in Section 5-70(1) of the Act. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>c)         Certification of Compliance with CE Requirements </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>1)         Each licensee shall certify, on the renewal application, full
compliance with the CE requirements set forth in subsections (a) and (b). </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>2)         The Division may, for purposes of compliance audits, require
additional evidence demonstrating compliance with the CE requirements.  Each
licensee shall retain proof of CE completed. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>3)         For purposes of a compliance audit, the Division shall accept
verification submitted directly from a licensed education provider on behalf of
a licensee as proof of CE compliance.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>4)         Failure to comply with CE requirements is a violation of the
Act and may subject a licensee to a citation or other discipline set forth in
the Act or this Part.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>d)         The Division shall conduct audits to verify compliance with
this Section (see Section 1450.960).  </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-autospace:none'>(Source: 
Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512, effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.460  Application for Broker License by
Endorsement </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>a)         Each
applicant for an Illinois broker license by endorsement shall submit to the
Division:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         An
application, in a format provided by the Division;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         The
required fee as set forth in Section 1450.130;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         <i>An
official statement from the licensing authority of each state or jurisdiction
in which the broker is licensed certifying:</i></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>A)        That
the broker <i>has an active license;</i></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>B)        <i>That
the broker is in good standing</i>; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>C)        <i>Any
history of discipline against the broker in that state or jurisdiction of
licensure</i>;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>4)         Proof
of passing an examination approved by the Division on <i>Illinois-specific real
estate brokerage laws</i>;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>5)         <i>Proof
of successful completion of a</i> 30-hour <i>pre-license endorsement course</i>
recommended by the Board and <i>approved by the Division</i> in accordance with
Section 5-60.5(b)(4) of the Act;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>6)         Proof
of sponsorship if the applicant is seeking an active license;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>7)         The
applicant's written designation appointing the Secretary to act as the broker's
agent upon whom all judicial and other process or legal notices directed to the
broker may be served in accordance with Sections 5-60.5(d) and (f) of the Act;
and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>8)         A
written attestation by the applicant in accordance with Section 5-60.5(c) of
the Act that, upon licensure, the applicant will abide by all provisions of the
Act with respect to their real estate activities within the State of Illinois
and submit to the jurisdiction of the Department.  [225 ILCS 454/5-60.5]</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>b)         <i>If
the broker has been actively practicing as a broker or its license equivalent
in any other state or jurisdiction for less than 2 years immediately prior to
the date of application, the broker must complete the 45 hours of post-license
education</i> by the first renewal pursuant to the Act and Section 1450.410. 
[225 ILCS 454/5-60.5]</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>c)         After
review by the Division for compliance with the Act and the Department of
Professional Regulation Law [20 ILCS 2105], the Division will issue an Illinois
broker license.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in'>(Source:  Added at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512,
effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>
<br><p class="subheading">SUBPART E:  MANAGING BROKER LICENSING AND EDUCATION</p>

<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.500  Managing
Broker Pre-License Education Requirements</b> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'>An applicant for a managing
broker license must have completed a total of 165 credit hours of instruction
as set forth in Section 1450.1105.  The 45-hour managing broker pre-license
education shall be valid for 2 years after satisfactory completion.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in'>(Source:  Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512,
effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.510  Managing
Broker Examination</b><s> </s></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>a)         Each applicant for a managing broker license shall submit an
application for examination on Illinois-specific brokerage laws as determined
by the Division or its designated testing service, including: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>1)         Certification that the applicant is at least 20 years of age; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>2)         Successful completion of high school or equivalency required
under Section 5-28 of the Act; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>3)         The fee required by Section 1450.130; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>4)         Proof of one of the following: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>A)        Currently admitted to practice law by the Supreme Court of
Illinois; or </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>B)        Completion of a total of 165 credit hours of instruction in
real estate courses approved by the Division. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>b)         If a person fails the examination four consecutive times, the person
must repeat the pre-license education requirement set forth in Section 5-28 of
the Act prior to taking the examination again and must complete a new
application for examination. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>c)         As set forth in Section 5-35 of the Act, the 2-year time
period after the completion of pre-license education during which that
education will be accepted applies to approved pre-license education. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-autospace:none'>(Source: 
Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512, effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.520  Application
for Managing Broker License by Examination</b> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>a)         Each applicant for a managing broker license shall submit to
the Division: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>1)         A signed and completed application in a format provided by the
Division; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>2)         The required fee set forth in Section 1450.130; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>3)         Proof of successful completion of the written examination on
Illinois-specific brokerage laws authorized by the Division; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>4)         Proof of sponsorship or self-sponsorship if the applicant is
seeking an active license (see Section 1450.110); </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>5)         A transcript, if applicable;  </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>6)         If the applicant will be a self-sponsored managing broker, a
completed consent to examine and audit special accounts form; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>7)         A certification that the applicant has been actively licensed
and in good standing as a broker for 2 consecutive years out of the last 3
years; and </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>8)         Any other documentation prescribed by the Division.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>b)         An applicant shall have one year from the date of receipt of a
passing examination score to submit an application with the Division and to
meet all of the requirements for licensure. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>c)         A self-sponsored managing broker shall comply with all the
office requirements set forth in Section 1450.610.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-autospace:none'>(Source: 
Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512, effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.530  Application
for Managing Broker License by Reciprocity</b> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>a)         Until December 31, 2025, an Illinois managing broker license shall
be issued by reciprocity to a managing broker under the following conditions: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>1)         The managing broker holds an active managing broker license,
or its equivalent, by examination in a state with a reciprocal agreement with
the Department; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>2)         The managing broker licensing standards of that state are
substantially equivalent to or greater than the minimum standards in Illinois; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>3)         The managing broker maintains a physical or virtual place of
business, as permitted by law, in the managing broker's state of licensure and
has been actively engaging in licensed activities as a managing broker or
broker during the immediately preceding 2 years; and </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>4)         The managing broker submits:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>A)        An application, in a format provided by the Division;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>B)        The required fee set forth in Section 1450.130; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>C)        A certification, or a statement bearing the seal of the
licensing authority, from each state of licensure, evidencing: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>i)          an active managing broker license in that state; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>ii)         that the license is in good standing; and </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>iii)        any disciplinary action taken against the managing broker. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>D)        Proof of passing an examination approved by the Division on Illinois-specific
real estate brokerage laws; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>E)        If the managing broker does not maintain a definite place of
business within the State of Illinois, a written statement that: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>i)          appoints the Secretary to act as the managing broker's agent
upon whom all judicial and other process may be served; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>ii)         acknowledges and agrees to abide by all of the provisions of
the Act and this Part with respect to all of the managing broker's licensed activities
within and relating to the State of Illinois; and </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>iii)        assents to jurisdiction of the Department; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>F)         If the managing broker is not self-sponsored, then proof of
sponsorship if the applicant is seeking an active license. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>b)         Any licensee who renews a license issued under a reciprocal
agreement assents to jurisdiction of the Department without regard to the
location of the licensee's domicile or principal place of business or office
locations. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>c)         All requirements for licensure by reciprocity shall be met
within one year after the date of original application, but no later than
December 31, 2025, or the application shall be denied, and the fee forfeited.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>d)         No applications for licensure as a managing broker pursuant to
a reciprocal agreement and this Section shall be submitted or granted on or
after January 1, 2026, regardless of whether the application is new or if it
seeks reconsideration of a prior application.  Licenses granted under
reciprocal agreements prior to January 1, 2026, shall remain in force and may
be renewed in the same manner as provided for a managing broker license in the
Act and this Part.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-autospace:none'>(Source: 
Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512, effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.540  Managing
Broker Continuing Education</b> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>a)         Continuing Education Hour Requirements </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>1)         As set forth in Section 5-70 of the Act, each managing broker,
except as otherwise provided in Section 5-70(b), is required to complete 12 CE hours
during the current term of the license.  The total of 12 CE hours of courses
approved by the Division may be taken at any time during the current term of
the license.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>2)         In addition to the CE required in subsection (a)(1), all renewing
managing brokers must complete a 12-hour broker management CE course, the
content of which shall be recommended by the Board and approved by the Division.
This 12-hour broker management CE course must be completed in the classroom, by
interactive webinar, or by online distance education format. In order to
promote uniformity and consistency, the Division, following the recommendation
of the Board, may provide a standardized curriculum to be utilized by all licensed
education providers.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>3)         Pursuant to Section 5-70(l) of the Act, a managing broker
renewing their license for the first time may use the 45-hour managing broker
pre-license brokerage administration and management course to satisfy the
12-hour broker management CE requirement.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>4)         If the current term of the managing broker license ends within
the 90 days after the initial managing broker license is issued, a licensee is
not required to complete CE for that managing broker license term but shall
complete all CE requirements before the next managing broker renewal deadline. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>5)         Managing brokers licensed in Illinois but residing and
practicing in other states shall comply with the CE requirements set forth in
this Section, unless they are exempt under Section 5-70(b) of the Act. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>6)         The Division shall conduct audits to verify compliance with
this Section. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>b)         Approved Continuing Education </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>1)         CE credit may be earned for verified attendance at,
participation in, or completion of a licensed course offered by a licensed education
provider that is approved pursuant to Sections 1450.1100 and 1450.1105. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>2)         CE credit, except broker management excluded pursuant to
Section 5-70(b) of the Act, may be earned by passing a proctored exam for a correspondence
or home study course offered and verified by a licensed education provider (see
Section 1450.1155). </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>3)         As set forth in Section 5-70 of the Act, CE credit shall be
obtained through completion of a single core curriculum, an elective curriculum,
and a 12-hour broker management CE curriculum recommended by the Board and approved
by the Division. CE requirements shall be deemed satisfied upon successful
completion of the following: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>A)        Core Curriculum. A minimum of 6 CE credit hours per license
term in a core curriculum course recommended by the Board and approved by the Division,
which must include at least 2 credit hours of fair housing training.  Pursuant
to Section 5-70, the core curriculum may also cover subjects including but not
limited to advertising, agency, agency disclosures, property disclosures, escrow,
fair housing, residential leasing agent management, and license law.<b><span
style='color:blue'> </span></b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>B)        Elective Curriculum. No fewer than 6 CE credit hours per license
term in elective curriculum courses approved by the Division, which may cover
subjects including but not limited to license law, escrow, antitrust, fair
housing, agency, appraisal, property management, residential brokerage, farm
property management, transaction management rights and duties of parties in a
transaction, commercial brokerage and leasing, real estate financing,
disclosures, residential leasing agent management, advertising, broker
supervision and designated managing broker responsibility, professional
conduct, use of technology, diversity equity and inclusion, and any training
required by law (e.g., sexual harassment prevention training (see 20 ILCS
2105/2105-15.5)) may be credited toward elective hours.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>C)        The 12-hour broker management CE course.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-41.85pt;text-autospace:
none'>4)           As set forth in Section 5-70(g) of the Act, CE credit may be
earned by a licensed instructor for teaching a licensed CE course. Credit for
teaching a licensed CE course may only be earned once for that course during a license
term. One hour of instruction is equal to one CE hour. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-41.85pt;text-autospace:
none'>5)           As set forth in Section 5-75 of the Act, if licensees earn
CE credit hours in another state or territory for which they will claim credit
toward compliance in Illinois, the licensee shall submit an application and a $50
fee within 90 days after completing the CE course and prior to expiration of
the license. The Division shall review and approve the CE course if the education
provider, CE course, and delivery method of assessing participation and
comprehension are substantially equivalent to those licensed in Illinois. In
determining whether the education provider and CE course are substantially
equivalent, the Division shall use the criteria in Sections 5-70 through 5-80
of the Act and this Section. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-41.85pt;text-autospace:
none'>6)           CE credit shall only be given for CE courses offered by education
providers licensed by the Division. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-41.85pt;text-autospace:
none'>7)           Pursuant to Section 5-70(j) of the Act, no more than 12
hours of CE credit may be taken in one calendar day.  If a proctored final exam
is required for a licensee to receive credit for the CE course or courses, the
exam may be given either at the end of each individual course or group of
courses.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-41.85pt;text-autospace:
none'>8)           CE credit shall not be given for pre-license and post-license
education courses, except as set forth in Section 5-70(1) of the Act.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>c)         Certification of Compliance with CE Requirements </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>1)         Each licensee shall certify on the renewal application full
compliance with the CE requirements set forth in subsections (a) and (b). </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>2)         The Division may, for purposes of compliance audits, require
additional evidence demonstrating compliance with the CE requirements.  Each
licensee shall retain proof of CE completed. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>3)         For purposes of a compliance audit, the Division shall accept
verification submitted directly from a licensed education provider on behalf of
a licensee as proof of CE compliance.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>4)         Failure to comply with CE requirements is a violation of the
Act and may subject a licensee to a citation or other discipline as set forth
in the Act or this Part.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>d)         The Division shall conduct audits to verify compliance with
this Section (see Section 1450.960).  </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-autospace:none'>(Source: 
Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512, effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.550  Managing Broker License Transfer to
Broker License</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>a)         An
individual licensee holding an active managing broker license may exchange the managing
broker license for a broker license if the managing broker license is eligible
for renewal. Upon issuance of the broker license, the licensee shall be subject
to all CE requirements (see Section 1450.450).</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>b)         To
transfer a managing broker license to a broker license an applicant shall
submit to the Division:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         A
signed and completed application in a format provided by the Division; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:.5in'>2)         The
required fee set forth in Section 1450.130(b)(5).</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>c)         Upon
the transfer of a managing broker license to a broker license, the managing
broker license shall be cancelled. Any prior discipline will be reflected in
the new broker license record. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>d)         To
obtain a managing broker license after transferring to a broker license, a
transferee shall meet the requirements of a new applicant for a managing broker
license as set forth in the Act and this Part. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in'>(Source:  Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512,
effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.560  Managing Broker Self-Sponsorship</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>a)         A
self-sponsored managing broker may sponsor licensees if the self-sponsored
managing broker meets all the requirements for a sponsoring broker.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>b)         A
sole proprietor must be licensed as a real estate managing broker.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>c)         Temporary
Practice</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         Within
15 days after the death or disability of a self-sponsored managing broker, a
written request for authorization allowing the continued operation of the
office may be submitted to the Division.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         The
Division will issue written authorization allowing the continued operation
provided that:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>A)        A
licensed managing broker assumes responsibility in writing and agrees to
supervise the operation of the office; or</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>B)        In the
case of the death or disability of a self-sponsored managing broker, an
authorized representative of the estate assumes responsibility in writing and
agrees to supervise the operation of the office.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         Written
authorization under subsection (c)(2) shall be valid for no more than 60 days
unless extended by the Division for good cause shown and upon written request
by the broker or authorized representative.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>4)         The
Division will honor the order of a court of competent jurisdiction or any other
legal documentation appointing a representative of the estate for the sole
purpose of closing out the affairs of a self-sponsored managing broker who is
deceased or disable, but not for the purpose of actively engaging in licensed
activities.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>5)         This
subsection (c) applies to a self-sponsored managing broker licensee who
sponsors other licensees.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>(Source:  Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512,
effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.570  Application for Managing Broker License
by Endorsement</b> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>a)         Each
applicant for an Illinois managing broker license by endorsement shall submit
to the Division:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         An
application, in a format provided by the Division;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         The required
fee as set forth in Section 1450.130;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         An
official statement from the licensing authority of each state or jurisdiction
in which the managing broker is licensed certifying:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>A)        <i>That
the managing broker has an active license</i>;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>B)        <i>That
the managing broker is in good standing</i>; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>C)        <i>Any
history of discipline against the managing broker in that state or jurisdiction
of licensure</i>; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>D)        No <i>less
than 2 years</i> of active practice <i>immediately</i> preceding <i>the date of
application</i>.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>4)         Proof
of passing an examination approved by the Division on <i>Illinois-specific real
estate brokerage laws</i>;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>5)         <i>Proof
of successful completion of</i> a 30-hour <i>pre-license endorsement course</i>
recommended by the Board and <i>approved by the Division</i> in accordance with
Section 5-60.5(a)(5) of the Act;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>6)         The
applicant's written designation appointing the Secretary to act as the broker's
agent upon whom all judicial and other process or legal notices directed to the
broker may be served in accordance with Sections 5-60.5(d) and (f) of the Act;
and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>7)         A
written attestation by the applicant in accordance with Section 5-60.5(c) of
the Act that, upon licensure, the applicant will abide by all provisions of the
Act with respect to their real estate activities within the State of Illinois
and submit to the jurisdiction of the Department.  [225 ILCS 454/5-60.5]</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>b)         After
review by the Division for compliance with the Act and the Department of
Professional Regulation Law [20 ILCS 2105], the Division will issue an Illinois
managing broker license.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in'>(Source:  Added at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512,
effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>
<br><p class="subheading">SUBPART F:  CORPORATIONS, LIMITED LIABILITY COMPANIES,  PARTNERSHIPS, LIMITED PARTNERSHIPS AND LIMITED LIABILITY PARTNERSHIPS</p>

<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.600  Application for Corporations, Limited
Liability Companies, Partnerships, Limited Partnerships and Limited Liability Partnerships
Licenses </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>a)         Each
applicant for a corporation, limited liability company, partnership, limited
partnership, or limited liability partnership license shall submit:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         A
signed and completed application in a format provided by the Division; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         The
required fee set forth in Section 1450.130;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         A
Federal Employer Identification Number (FEIN); </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>4)         Consent
for the Division to examine and audit special accounts; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>5)         The
name and license number of the designated managing broker;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>6)         If an
assumed name will be used, a copy of the certificate authorizing it to do
business, as set forth in the Assumed Business Name Act [805 ILCS 405];</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>7)         Proof
of ownership that may, from time to time, be required by the Division; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>8)         Physical
or virtual address of principal place of business in Illinois.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>b)         All
requirements for a licensee to engage in licensed activities as a corporation,
limited liability company, partnership, limited partnership, or limited
liability partnership shall be met within one year after the date of original
application or the application shall be denied, and the fee forfeited.
Thereafter, to be considered for licensure, the applicant shall submit a new
application with the required fee. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>c)         Corporations,
in addition to the items listed in subsection (a), shall submit the following: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         A
signed and completed application containing the name of the corporation, the
address of its principal place of business, a list of all officers' names, and the
license number for each officer who is licensed as a broker or managing broker
in Illinois; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         A
copy of the Articles of Incorporation bearing the seal of the office, in the
jurisdiction in which the corporation is organized, whose duty it is to register
corporations under the laws of that jurisdiction. In addition, a foreign
corporation must submit a copy of the certificate of authority to transact
business in this State filed with the SOS; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         All
unlicensed officers shall submit affidavits of non-participation in a format
provided by the Division; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>4)         A
list of all shareholders, the number of shares of the corporation owned by each
shareholder and the license number for each shareholder who is a licensee in
Illinois; and </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>5)         No
corporation shall be granted a license if any non-participating owner or
officer is currently barred from real estate practice because of a suspended or
revoked license. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>d)         Limited
liability companies, in addition to the items listed in subsection (a), shall
submit the following: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         A
signed and completed application containing the name of the limited liability
company and the address of its principal place of business; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>A)        If
member-managed, a list of all members' names and the license number for each
member who is licensed as a broker or managing broker in Illinois; or</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>B)        If
manager-managed, a list of all managers' names and the license number for each
manager who is licensed as a broker or managing broker in Illinois;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         If a
member or manager is an entity, the applicant shall provide a list of all
members' or managers' names and the license number for each owner, officer,
manager, member, or partner of the entity who is licensed as a broker or
managing broker in Illinois; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         A
copy of the Articles of Organization filed with the SOS.  In addition, a
foreign limited liability company must submit a copy of the application for
admission to transact business filed with the SOS; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>4)         All
unlicensed members, including a member entity's owner, officer, member, or
partner, shall submit affidavits of non-participation in a format provided by
the Division; and </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>5)         No
limited liability company shall be granted a license if any non-participating
member or manager is currently barred from real estate practice because of a suspended
or revoked license. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>e)         Partnerships,
in addition to the items listed in subsection (a), shall submit the following: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         A
signed and completed application containing the name of the partnership, the
address of its principal place of business, a list of all general partners'
names, and the license number for each general partner who is licensed as a
broker or managing broker in Illinois; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         An
affidavit stating that the partnership has been legally formed; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         If a
sponsoring broker, acting as a partnership, operates under any name other than
that appearing on the sponsoring broker's license, the sponsoring broker shall
submit to the Division a copy of the filing or certificate authorizing it to do
business under an assumed name from the SOS and a certified copy of the
sponsoring broker's registration under the Assumed Business Name Act [805 ILCS
405]. The assumed business name registration shall be obtained in each county
in which the assumed business name is used;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>4)         All
unlicensed partners shall submit affidavits of non-participation in a format
provided by the Division; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>5)         No
partnership shall be granted a license if any non-participating partner is
currently barred from real estate practice because of a suspended or revoked
license.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>f)         Limited
partnerships or limited liability partnerships, in addition to the items listed
in subsection (a), shall submit the following: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         A
signed and completed application containing the name of the limited partnership
or limited liability partnership, the address of its principal place of
business, a list of all partners' names, and the license number for each
partner who is licensed as a broker or managing broker in Illinois<span
style='letter-spacing:-.05pt'>. </span>If a partner is an entity, the applicant
shall <span style='letter-spacing:-.05pt'>provide </span>a list of all
partners' names and the license number <span style='letter-spacing:-.05pt'>for
each</span> owner, officer, manager, member, or partner of the entity who is
licensed as a broker or managing broker in Illinois; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         A
letter of authority from the SOS or, if it is a foreign limited partnership or
foreign limited liability partnership, a copy of the application for admission
endorsed by the SOS; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         All
unlicensed partners shall submit affidavits of non-participation in a format
provided by the Division; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>4)         No
limited partnership or limited liability partnership shall be granted a license
if any non-participating partner is currently barred from real estate practice
because of a suspended or revoked license.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>g)         After
receipt and approval of the application, required fee and appropriate
documentation, the Division shall issue a license to the corporation, limited
liability company, partnership, limited partnership, or limited liability
partnership, or shall notify the applicant of the basis for denial. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in'>(Source:  Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512,
effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.610  Place of
Business; Office and Virtual Office Requirements</b> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal>A licensee's place of business shall be the physical office
or virtual office of the licensee's sponsoring broker. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>a)         Each
sponsoring broker shall register all physical or virtual office locations with
the Division in a format provided by the Division.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>b)         Physical
Offices</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         An
office is any fixed business location or physical structure that is owned,
controlled, operated, or maintained by a licensee who, at that business
location or structure, is: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>A)        Engaging
in licensed activities; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>B)        Offering
real estate services to consumers; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>C)        Holding
out to the public that the licensee is engaging in licensed activities; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>D)        Maintaining
original real estate documents and records related to active or pending
transactions; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>E)        Maintaining
current escrow records;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>F)         Maintaining
any record related to special accounts; or</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>G)        Meeting
consumers for the purpose of engaging in licensed activities. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         The
following places do not constitute a physical or fixed office. These places are
provided as examples and are not intended to be inclusive or exclusive of other
places: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>A)        A
motor vehicle primarily used for transportation; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>B)        A
place that is solely devoted to advertising real estate matters of a general
nature or to making a sponsoring broker's business name generally known (e.g.,
trade show, expo); </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>C)        A
place that a licensee uses solely for storage or archiving of records; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>D)        A
licensee's residence, unless it is held out to the public as a location where
the person is engaging in licensed activities and satisfies all other
requirements for an office; or</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>E)        A post
office box, mail drop location or other similar facility. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>c)         Virtual
Offices</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         A
virtual office is an office from which real estate brokerage services are
provided, without a dedicated office space or fixed physical location, under
the supervision of the designated managing broker, and that is owned,
controlled, operated, or maintained by a licensee who is:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>A)        Engaging
in licensed activities;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>B)        Offering
real estate services to consumers;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>C)        Holding
out to the public that the licensee is engaging in licensed activities;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>D)        Maintaining
real estate documents and records related to active, pending and completed
transactions, securely stored in the format in which they were originally
created, sent, or received.  Documents originally created in physical form may
be converted to an electronic format only if the content is not altered;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>E)        Maintaining
current escrow records that are securely stored in the format in which they
were originally created, sent, or received;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>F)         Maintaining
any record related to special accounts, securely stored in the format in which
they were originally created, sent, or received;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         A
nonresident broker who is licensed in Illinois by examination or pursuant to
the provisions of Section 5-60 or 5-60.6 of the Act may operate a virtual
office in the State subject to all requirements of the Act and this Part.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         A
virtual office shall have a digital infrastructure facilitating the transaction
of business, communications, advertising, and real estate services through a
virtual office website, URL, or other digital platform.  Through this website
or digital platform, the general public is invited to transact business and it
is utilized to conduct the communication, advertising, and real estate services
related to that business.  The virtual website, URL or digital platform shall
be registered in a format provided by the Division and shall:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>A)        Display
a current registry of all sponsored licensees and their corresponding license
numbers registered with the Division as set forth in Section 1450.730;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>B)        Display
the city or geographic location and state where real estate brokerage services
are offered;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>C)        Display
contact information for the designated managing broker, including name, license
number, valid phone number and email address.  If the sponsoring broker has
multiple designated managing brokers, display each designated managing broker's
contact information and the name and license number of the licensees they
supervise;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>D)        Maintain
a secure intranet or secure portal that is accessible by employees, independent
contractors, licensees and, upon request, the Division.  The intranet or portal
shall include the following:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>i)          Electronic
records, securely stored in the format they were originally created, sent, or
received, and made accessible for inspection by the Division in a secure
electronic format.  Documents originally created in physical form may be
converted to an electronic format only if the content is not altered;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>ii)         An
updated registry of sponsored licensees, their designated managing brokers,
