none'>C)        Transaction records shall be maintained for 5 years.  The
sponsoring broker shall ensure that any transaction records involving active or
pending transactions or representation, or transactions in which escrow funds
or moneys belonging to others were received and have not yet been disbursed for
the immediate prior 2 years shall be maintained in the office or place of
business.  All transaction records maintained at the office shall be made
available for inspection and audit during normal business hours by the Division
staff no later than 24 hours after a request for escrow records and related
documents.  Any transaction records more than 2 years old and stored at a
location other than the sponsoring broker's office, whether physical or virtual,
shall be made available for inspection during normal business hours, through
physical or electronic means, as soon as available but no later than 30 days
after the request.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>D)        Sponsoring brokers may allow their sponsored licensees to
maintain duplicate transaction records. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>4)         Employment or independent contractor agreements required by
Section 10-20 of the Act shall be maintained for 5 years after the sponsored
licensee is no longer associated with the sponsoring broker.  The sponsoring broker
shall maintain a written employment or independent contractor agreement for
every licensee who is employed by or associated with the sponsoring broker.  A
copy of the employment or independent contractor agreement for each sponsored
licensee shall be maintained at the sponsoring broker's office location registered
with the Division. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>5)         Records reflecting the payment of compensation for the
performance of licensed activities shall be maintained for 5 years. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>b)         All records may be kept in physical or electronic form. If the
records are kept electronically, the sponsoring broker shall ensure that a back-up
is made at reasonable intervals, but at least once a month, so as to protect
the data.  Back-ups can be kept either at the sponsoring broker's office or
offsite. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>c)         Any disclosure required by the Act, or this Part may be
provided in physical form or, if agreed to by both parties, in electronic
format and may incorporate electronic signatures.  Copies of all disclosures,
whether in physical or electronic form, must be retained by the sponsoring
broker. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in'>d)         If escrow records are
lost, stolen, or destroyed, the sponsoring broker must: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         Report
the loss to the Division's enforcement division within 48 hours; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         Immediately
obtain copies of monthly bank statements, deposit and disbursement receipts,
and any other available records, to reconstruct the escrow records. </p>

<p class=JCARSourceNote> </p>

<p class=JCARSourceNote style='margin-left:.5in'>(Source:  Amended at 49 Ill.
Reg. 9512, effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b> </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.760  Disclosure
of Compensation</b> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'>Pursuant to Section 10-10(c) of
the Act, a licensee shall disclose, in writing, either physically or
electronically, any compensation the licensee expects to receive or that the
licensee's sponsoring broker will receive that arises out of a referral to any
person or entity in which the licensee or licensee's sponsoring broker has any
interest greater than 1%.  This provision applies when the compensation is for
the purpose of the client obtaining services related to the transaction,
including any financial institution, insurance broker, mortgage broker, home
inspector or any other third party services.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in;text-autospace:none'>(Source: 
Amended at 45 Ill. Reg. 2851, effective February 23, 2021)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.765  Disclosure
of Licensee Status</b> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'>A licensee who sells, leases, or
purchases any property or any interest in a property that is the subject of the
transaction, whether directly or indirectly, for purposes of Section 10-27 of
the Act, must disclose, in writing to all parties to the transaction, their
licensure status prior to initiating a transaction when the licensee: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>a)         Is selling, leasing, or seeking to purchase real estate as
sole owner, tenant in common, joint tenant, or tenant by the entirety; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>b)         Holds a beneficial interest in a land trust selling, leasing,
or seeking to purchase an interest in the subject real estate; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>c)         Is a partner in a partnership, limited partnership or limited
liability partnership selling, leasing, or seeking to purchase an interest in
the subject real estate; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>d)         Is an officer, director and/or shareholder of a corporation, other
than a publicly traded corporation, selling, leasing, or seeking to purchase an
interest in the subject real estate; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>e)         Is a manager or member of a limited liability company selling,
leasing, or seeking to purchase an interest in the subject real estate; or </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>f)         Has any direct or indirect interest in the subject real
estate.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>(Source:  Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512, effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.770  Brokerage Agreements and Property
Management Agreements </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>a)         A
sponsoring broker entering into a brokerage relationship shall set forth the
terms of that relationship in a written brokerage agreement pursuant to Section
15-50 of the Act.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>b)         Exclusive
brokerage agreements, including exclusive listing agreements and exclusive
buyer or tenant brokerage agreements, shall:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         Be in
writing and shall indicate the minimum services that must be provided as set
forth in Section 15-75 of the Act.  For purposes of this Section, "in
writing" or "written" means physical or electronic writing. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         Failure
to include language in a brokerage agreement providing for minimum services as
set forth in Section 15-75 of the Act or language waiving those minimum
services will, under the definition of "exclusive brokerage
agreement" in Section 1-10 of the Act, result in the brokerage agreement
being considered to be non-exclusive.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         A
licensee may discuss a possible future brokerage agreement with a consumer
whose property is exclusively listed with another sponsoring broker or who is
subject to a written exclusive buyer or tenant brokerage agreement only if:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in'>A)        The consumer initiates
the contact; or</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in'>B)        The following occurs:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>i)          The
licensee makes a request in writing, mailed or emailed, to the sponsoring
broker who has the exclusive agreement for the expiration date of the agreement
between the consumer and the sponsoring broker who has the exclusive brokerage
agreement;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>ii)         The
licensee who has the exclusive brokerage agreement fails to provide a response
in writing, mailed or emailed, within 10 calendar days;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>iii)        The
information from the broker or sponsoring broker who has the exclusive
brokerage agreement is not received within 14 calendar days; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>iv)        The
requested information cannot be obtained by the licensee from another source of
shared broker information.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>c)         Written
buyer or tenant brokerage agreements, whether exclusive or non-exclusive, shall
contain the following: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>1)         Agreed basis or amount
of compensation and time of payment; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         Name
of the sponsoring broker, designated agents, and the buyers or tenants; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         Signatures
of the sponsoring broker, the buyers, tenants, or authorized signatories on
behalf of the buyers or tenants; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>4)         Duties
of the buyer or tenant's broker, or duties of the residential leasing agent;
and </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>5)         The
duration of the brokerage agreement, including an automatic expiration date, or
if longer than one year, the right to terminate the agreement annually with 30
days prior written notice; and </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>d)         Written
listing agreements, whether exclusive or non-exclusive, shall contain the
following: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>1)         List price; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         Agreed
basis or amount of commission and the time of payment of the commission and any
amounts paid to cooperating brokers who represent other parties to a
transaction; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         Name
of the sponsoring broker, designated agents, and owners of the real property; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>4)         Identification
of the real property involved (address and/or legal description); </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>5)         Signatures
of the sponsoring broker and owners or an authorized signatory on behalf of the
owners; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>6)         Duties of the listing
broker or residential leasing agent; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>7)         The
duration of the listing agreement, including an automatic expiration date, or
if longer than one year, the right to terminate the agreement annually with 30
days prior written notice.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>e)         Written
property management agreements shall be required if duties performed include
licensed activity as defined by "broker" in Section 1-10 of the Act.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>f)         Written
property management agreements shall at a minimum contain the following:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         Identification
of the real property involved (address and/or legal description);</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         Name
of the sponsoring broker, designated agents, and owners of the real property;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         Signatures
of the sponsoring broker and owners or an authorized signatory on behalf of the
owners;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:.5in'>4)         Duties
of the broker managing the property and of the owner;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:.5in'>5)         List
price;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>6)         Agreed
payment or amount of compensation and the timing of payments, and any amounts
paid to cooperating brokers, including those who represent other parties to the
transaction;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>7)         The
duration of the property management agreement, including an automatic
expiration date, or if longer than one year, the right to terminate the
agreement annually with 30 days prior written notice.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>g)         Licensees
shall enter into a written brokerage agreement with a client as soon as
reasonably practical but no later than:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>1)         showing or marketing
the property of a seller or owner; or </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>2)         prior to initiating the
purchase or lease of a property for a buyer or tenant.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>h)         Written
brokerage agreements shall expressly provide that no amendment or alteration to
the terms, with respect to the amount of commission or with respect to the time
of payment of commission, shall be valid or binding unless made in writing and
signed by the parties. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>i)          No
licensee shall use real estate contract forms to change previously agreed-upon
commission payment terms. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>j)          If a
listing agreement states that, in the event of a default by a buyer, the sponsoring
broker's full commission or fees will be paid out of an earnest money deposit,
with any remaining earnest money to be paid to the seller, the provision shall
appear in the listing agreement in letters larger than those generally used in
the listing agreement. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>k)         Each
brokerage agreement shall clearly state that it is illegal for either the owner
or any licensee to refuse to show, display, lease or sell to any person because
of, race, color, religion, national origin, sex, ancestry, age, marital status,
physical or mental disability, familial status, pregnancy, sexual orientation,
including, but not limited to, gender identity, unfavorable discharge from the
military service, military status, order of protection status, an arrest record,
immigration status, source of income, or any other class protected by Article 3
of the Illinois Human Rights Act. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>l)          Each
brokerage agreement for a residential property of 4 units or less that provides
for a protection period subsequent to its termination date shall also provide
that no commission or fee will be due and owing pursuant to the terms of the
brokerage agreement if, during the protection period, a valid, written
brokerage agreement is entered into with another sponsoring broker. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>(Source:  Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512,
effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.775  Written
Agreements</b> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>a)         No licensee shall solicit, accept, or execute any contract or
other document relating to a real estate transaction that contains any blanks
with the intention of filling them in after the parties have signed or initialed
the contract or other document.  This shall not be construed to mean that there
will never be a blank space on a contract or any other document. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>b)         No licensee shall make any addition to, deletion from or
alteration of any signed contract or other document relating to a real estate
transaction without the written consent or direction from all signatories.  No
licensee shall process any contract or other document that has been altered
after being signed, unless each addition, deletion or alteration is signed or
initialed by all signatories at the time of the addition, deletion, or
alteration. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>c)         A true copy of the original or corrected contract or other
document relating to a real estate transaction shall be delivered within 24
hours after the time of signing or initialing the original or correction to the
person signing or initialing the contract or other document. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>d)         All
forms used by licensees intended to become binding real estate contracts shall
clearly state that fact in the heading in large bold type.  No licensee shall
use a form designated Offer to Purchase when it is intended that the form shall
be a binding real estate contract.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>e)         This Section
applies to all written agreements, whether physical or electronic.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>(Source: 
Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512, effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b> </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.780  Referral
Fees and Affinity Relationships</b> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>a)         No licensee may pay a referral fee to an unlicensed person who
is not a principal to the transaction.  Any person receiving a referral fee must
be duly licensed as a residential leasing agent, broker or managing broker in Illinois
or hold an equivalent license of another state or country of domicile.  If the
person's country of domicile does not have a licensing statute for licensees, then
in order to receive a referral fee, the person must comply with the laws, if
any, of the country of domicile concerning the practice of real estate. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>b)         Request for Referral Fee</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>1)         No licensee may request a referral fee unless reasonable cause
for payment of the referral fee exists.  Reasonable cause for payment of a
referral fee means: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>A)        An actual introduction of a client was made to a licensee; or </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-autospace:none'>B)        A
contractual referral fee relationship exists with the licensee. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'><span
style='color:black'>2)         </span>No residential leasing agent licensee may
request, or be paid, a referral fee, except for a referral fee from a lease or
rental of residential real estate.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal><span style='color:black'> </span></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>3)         The fact that reasonable cause to demand a referral fee exists
does not necessarily mean that a legal right to the referral fee exists. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>4)         A licensee is prohibited from interfering with the agency
relationship of another licensee or attempting to induce a client to break an
exclusive listing or an exclusive representation agreement with another
licensee for the purpose of replacing that agreement with a new listing or
representation agreement in order to obtain a referral fee.  For purposes of
this Section, an agency relationship shall be deemed to exist when a written,
exclusive agency agreement (either a listing or buyer representation agreement)
is entered into.  Interfering with the agency relationship of another licensee
includes, but is not limited to: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>A)        Demanding a referral fee from another licensee without
reasonable cause; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>B)        Threatening to take harmful action against the client of
another licensee because of their existing agency relationship and in order to
obtain a referral fee; or </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>C)        Counseling the client of another licensee on how to terminate
or amend an existing agency agreement in order to obtain a referral fee. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>5)         Communicating corporate relocation policies or benefits to a
transferring employee, as long as that communication does not involve advice or
encouragement on how to terminate or amend an existing agency agreement, shall
not be considered interference under subsection (b)(4). </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in;text-autospace:none'>(Source: 
Amended at 45 Ill. Reg. 2851, effective February 23, 2021)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.785  Rental Finding Services </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>a)         Definition and
Application </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         A
rental finding service is any business that finds, attempts to find or offers
to find, for any person who pays or is obligated to pay a fee or other valuable
consideration, a unit of rental real estate or a lessee to occupy a unit of
rental real estate not owned or leased by the rental finding service. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         Any
person, corporation, limited liability company, partnership, limited
partnership, or limited liability partnership that operates a rental finding
service shall be considered a licensee, obtain the appropriate license, and
comply with this Section. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         This
Section shall not apply to persons exempted by Section 5-20 of the Act. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>b)         Contract.
 A rental finding service shall, prior to accepting a fee or other valuable
consideration for services, enter into a written contract with the person for
whom services are to be performed and deliver to the person a physical or
electronic copy of the contract.  The contract shall include, in the case of a
rental finding service that finds, offers, or attempts to find a unit of rental
real estate for a person, at a minimum, the following: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>1)         The term of the
contract; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         The
total amount to be paid for the services to be performed and a clear
designation of the amount, if any, paid in advance of the performance of the
services; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         A
statement regarding whether the fee paid in advance is refundable or
non-refundable, including the following in uniform type of a size larger than
that used for the balance of the contract: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>A)        Precise
conditions, if any, upon which a refund is based; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>B)        The
conditions shall occur within 90 days from the date of the contract; and </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>C)        The
refund shall be paid no later than 10 days after demand, provided the payment
of the fee has been honored; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>4)         The
type of rental unit desired, the geographical area requested, and the rent the
prospective tenant is willing to pay; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>5)         A
detailed statement of rental finding services to be performed by the licensee,
which shall include, at a minimum, the delivery to the prospective tenant of
all rental information set forth in subsection (c); </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>6)         A
statement that the contract shall be null and void if information concerning
possible rental units or locations furnished by the licensee is not current or
accurate with respect to the type of rental unit desired and as set forth in
subsection (b)(4).  A listing for a rental unit that has not been available for
rent for over 2 days shall be prima facie proof of not being current; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>7)         A
statement that information furnished by the licensee concerning possible rental
units may be up to 2 days old; and </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>8)         A
statement requiring the licensee to refund all fees paid in connection with the
contract if the contract is null and void for any reason.  The licensee shall
not impose any condition for the refund, and the contract shall state when the
refund will be paid. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>c)         Disclosure.
 As required by subsection (b)(5), the following information for each rental
unit the rental finding service is listing shall be provided, in writing, to
the person with whom the contract is entered: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         The
name, address, email address, and telephone number of the owner of each rental
unit or the owner's authorized agent; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         A
description of the rental unit; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         The
amount of rent requested; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>4)         The
amount of security deposit required; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>5)         A
statement describing utilities that are located in the rental unit and included
in the rent; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>6)         The
occupancy date and the term of lease; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>7)         A
statement setting forth the source of the information disclosed (i.e., owner, owner's
authorized agent); and </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>8)         All
other conditions that may reasonably be expected to be of concern to the
prospective tenant. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>d)         Permission
of Owner.  A rental finding service shall not list or advertise any rental unit
without the express written authority of the owner or owner's authorized agent
of each unit.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>(Source: 
Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512, effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.790  Broker Price Opinions and Comparative
Market Analyses </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>a)         An
individual who is a licensed managing broker or broker and also a licensed
appraiser as set forth in the Real Estate Appraiser Licensing Act of 2002 [<span
style='color:black;background:white'>225 ILCS 458] must include the </span>broker's
or managing broker's name and license number, not the appraiser's license
number, <span style='color:black;background:white'>on the initial page of the </span>broker
price opinion or comparative market analysis. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>b)         A
broker or managing broker preparing a broker price opinion or comparative
market analysis that does not comply with Section 10-45 of the Act is subject
to discipline for:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:.5in'>1)         False,
untruthful, or misleading statements; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         Inadequate
or improper preparation of a broker price opinion or comparative market
analysis; or</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:.5in'>3)         Any
violation set forth in Section 20-20 of the Act.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>(Source:  Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512,
effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>
<br><p class="subheading">SUBPART H:  AGENCY RELATIONSHIPS</p>

<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b> </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.800 
Confidentiality</b> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'>Licensees in receipt of
confidential information must take reasonable steps to safeguard that
information from unauthorized disclosure. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-autospace:none'>(Source: 
Amended at 45 Ill. Reg. 2851, effective February 23, 2021)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b> </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.810  Failure to
Disclose Information Not Affecting Physical Condition</b> <b>of Real Estate</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'>This Section is intended to
apply to actions taken by the Department under the Act and to all civil actions
in Illinois.  No cause of action shall arise against a licensee for the failure
to disclose: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>a)         That an occupant of the real estate was afflicted with Human
Immunodeficiency Virus (HIV) or any other medical condition; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>b)         That the real estate was the site of an act or occurrence that
had no effect on the physical condition of the property or its environment or
the structures located on that real estate, as set forth in Section 15-20 of
the Act.  These acts shall include, but are not limited to, murder or suicide; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>c)         Fact situations regarding real estate that is not the subject
of the transaction; or </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>d)         Physical conditions located on real estate that is not the
subject of the transaction and do not have a substantial adverse effect on the
value of the real estate that is the subject of the transaction. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>(Source:  Amended at 45 Ill. Reg. 2851, effective February 23, 2021)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b> </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.820  Dual Agency
Prohibition</b> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal>A licensee shall not serve as a dual agent in any
transaction when the licensee, or an entity in which the licensee has or will
have any ownership interest, directly or indirectly, is a party to the transaction.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>(Source:  Amended at 45 Ill. Reg. 2851,
effective February 23, 2021)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal><b> </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.830  Disclosure of Contemporaneous Offers</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b> </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal>"Contemporaneous offers" under Section 15-15(b) of
the Act shall be offers to purchase or lease on behalf of 2 or more clients
represented by the same designated agent for the same real estate parcel or
unit that the designated agent knows or has reason to know will be taken under
consideration by the owners or owners' representative at the same time.  If
there are contemporaneous offers from 2 or more clients of a designated agent, the
designated agent shall provide written disclosure, physically or
electronically, to those clients and, upon request by the clients, refer the
clients to another designated agent. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>(Source:  Amended at 45 Ill. Reg. 2851,
effective February 23, 2021)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>
<br><p class="subheading">SUBPART I:  DISCIPLINE RULES AND PROCEDURES</p>

<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.900 
Unprofessional Conduct</b> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'>Conduct that constitutes
dishonorable, unethical, or unprofessional conduct of a character likely to
deceive, defraud or harm the public includes, but is not limited to: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>a)         Failure to act in the best interests of a client. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>b)         Deliberately misleading a client as to the market value of
property. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>c)         Failing to advertise the property pursuant to the terms of the
listing agreement. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>d)         Deliberately misrepresenting to prospective purchasers or
their agents the condition of property or the availability of access to show
the property. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>e)         Purchasing or transferring property through an intermediary in
order to conceal the purchase by the licensee. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>f)         Inducing a seller to list property through false
representations. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>g)         Inducing a seller through false representations or false
promises to transfer property to the licensee. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>h)         Taking unfair advantage of a client's or customer's age,
disability, or lack of understanding of the English language. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>i)          Engaging in licensed activities with the public or other real
estate licensees in a manner that is abusive, harassing, or lewd. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>j)          Representing oneself as a sponsoring broker or designated managing
broker without providing actual supervision and management of the real estate entity
or licensees. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>k)         Failing to reasonably safeguard confidential information or
improperly using confidential information. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>l)          Obstructing an inspection, audit, investigation, examination,
or disciplinary proceeding. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>m)        Violation of Section 1450.750 (Special Accounts). </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>n)         Assisting or inducing a licensee or unlicensed individual to
violate the Act or this Part. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>o)         Any
conduct constituting a breach of duty to the client causing harm to the client
in the future.  In establishing harm, the Department need not prove actual economic
damage to the client.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>p)         Use
of a managing broker license to permit or enable a broker, residential leasing
agent, or other individual to operate or manage a licensed real estate entity
without actual participation in and control of that entity by the designated
managing broker.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>q)         Accessing
a property or granting permission to access a property without proper
authorization.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>(Source: 
Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512, effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.905  Temporary
Suspension</b> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal>The Secretary may temporarily suspend a license without a
hearing, simultaneously with the institution of proceedings for a hearing
provided for in Section 20-60 of the Act, if the Secretary finds evidence indicating
that the public interest, safety, or welfare imperatively requires emergency action.
 Emergency action is imperative when a licensee's conduct poses a threat that
the public's or another licensee's money or other property will be stolen or embezzled
or that continued licensure of a licensee will be a threat to the safety of the
public or another licensee.  </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>a)         The Department
may consider any one or more of the following acts committed by a licensee as
cause for temporary suspension, including, but not limited to: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>1)         Failure to account for or to remit any moneys or documents
that belong to others as set forth in Section 20-20(a)(16) of the Act; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>2)         Failure to maintain and deposit in a special or escrow
account, separate and apart from personal and other business accounts, all
escrow moneys belonging to others entrusted to a designated managing broker or
sponsoring broker while acting as a licensee, escrow agent or temporary
custodian of the funds of others, as set forth in Section 20-20(a)(17) of the
Act; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>3)         Failure to make escrow records and related documents for the
immediately preceding 2 years available, within 24 hours after request, to the
Division during normal business hours pursuant to Section 20-20(a)(18) of the
Act and Section 1450.755.  This action alone may not be sufficient grounds for
a temporary suspension; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>4)         Failure to make escrow records and related documents more than
2 years old available, within 30 days after request, to the Division during
normal business hours, in physical or electronic form, as set forth in Section
20-20(a)(27) of the Act and Section 1450.755; or</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>5)         Commingling money or property of others with the licensee's
own money or property, as set forth in Section 20-20(a)(22) of the Act. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>b)         A petition for temporary suspension shall: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>1)         State the statutory basis for the action petitioned; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>2)         Allege facts, supported by sufficient evidence; and </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>3)         Be presented to the Secretary. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>c)         An order for temporary suspension shall: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>1)         Contain sufficient notice regarding the basis for the action; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>2)         Recite the statutory basis for the action; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>3)         Demand immediate surrender of the license; and </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>4)         Be signed by the Secretary. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>d)         A notice of temporary suspension shall accompany the order and
shall: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>1)         Set a hearing date within 30 days after the date on which the
order takes effect; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>2)         Identify the location where the hearing will take place; and </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>3)         Provide information as to where the licensee may obtain the Department's
Rules of Practice in Administrative Hearings (68 Ill. Adm. Code 1110). </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in;text-autospace:none'>(Source: 
Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512, effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.910  Non-Disciplinary
Action</b> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'>Non-disciplinary action may
include, but is not limited to:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>a)         Issuance of a citation for failure to complete, or untimely
completion of, required CE;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>b)         Restricting a licensee's access to escrow funds; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>c)         Requiring the successful completion of licensed real estate courses,
including courses for those licensees who would otherwise not be required by
Section 5-70 of the Act to complete CE; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>d)         Requiring the licensee to provide any report, record or
document regarding licensed activities the Department deems relevant and
appropriate; or</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>e)         Imposition of an administrative fee.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>(Source:  Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512, effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.915  Suspension
or Revocation of a Sponsoring Broker or Designated Managing Broker License</b> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>a)         Suspension or Revocation of Sponsoring Broker </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-autospace:none'>Upon the
effective date of a suspension or revocation of the license of a sponsoring
broker or sole proprietorship: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>1)         The licenses of all sponsored licensees automatically become
inactive.  Each managing broker licensee may resume licensed activities only
upon securing a new sponsorship, either as a self-sponsored managing broker or
by another sponsoring broker.  Each broker or residential leasing agent may
resume licensed activities only upon securing a sponsorship with another
sponsoring broker. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>2)         Suspensions or revocations of a sponsoring broker shall not
have an effect on the enforceability of any pending, executed real estate
contracts. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>A)        The sponsoring broker whose license has been suspended or
revoked shall send a written notice, in physical or electronic form, to all
clients with a pending, executed real estate contract stating that the
sponsoring broker's license has been suspended or revoked and that the suspension
or revocation shall not have an effect on any pending, executed real estate contracts. 
The notice shall also identify the name, address, email, and telephone number
of the person in control of the escrow money.  To the extent clients require
additional real estate services, the notice shall provide that the clients may
seek real estate services from another sponsoring broker. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>B)        A suspension or revocation shall not preclude the receipt of
any commission or other compensation earned by the suspended or revoked
sponsoring broker or other formerly sponsored licensee prior to the effective
date of the suspension or revocation of the sponsoring broker. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>3)         If a broker's suspension or revocation directly relates to a
transaction for which compensation was earned, and the broker already received the
compensation related to the transaction leading to the suspension or revocation
of the license, the Department or Board may consider that fact when imposing
discipline.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>b)         Suspension or Revocation of a Designated Managing Broker</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-autospace:none'>In the event
of a suspension or revocation of a designated managing broker license, offices may
operate for 15 days without a replacement designated managing broker.  Within
15 days after a suspension or revocation of a designated managing broker
license, if a replacement designated managing broker has not been secured or a
written request for authorization to continue operation has not been submitted
to the Division, the offices must cease licensed activities. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>(Source:  Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512, effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.920  Inspections
and Audits</b> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>a)         Inspections  </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>1)         The Division is authorized to inspect areas of a sponsoring
broker's office that are open and generally available to the public at any time
during normal business hours, with or without the sponsoring broker's consent. 
The Division, with at least 24 hours' notice, may conduct a visual and physical
inspection of the non-public areas of a sponsoring broker's office and
interview any person, including any licensee or non-licensee, who may have
knowledge or information about the licensee's real estate practice, with or
without the consent of either the sponsoring broker or designated managing
broker.  The licensee may have an attorney present.  The Division's action will
not be postponed due to a licensee's attorney's unreasonable delay.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         The
Division is authorized to inspect a virtual office of the sponsoring broker,
with or without the sponsoring broker's consent. The Division, with at least 24
hours' notice, may conduct an inspection of the digital infrastructure and all
electronic records in the sponsoring broker's virtual office, and interview any
person, including any licensee or non-licensee who may have knowledge or
information about the licensee's real estate practice, with or without consent
of either the sponsoring broker or designated managing broker. The licensee may
have an attorney present. The Division's action will not be postponed due to a
licensee's attorney's unreasonable delay.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         Upon
any written or oral request by the Division for documents in physical or
electronic form, a licensee shall, except as otherwise allowed by subsection
(b), produce the requested documents within 30 days after the request. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>b)         Escrow Audits  </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-autospace:none'>The Division
is authorized to audit special accounts, escrow records and documents related
to any escrow accounts maintained by the licensee.  Any records or documents
may be provided in physical or electronic form.  Escrow audits may be conducted
at any time with the sponsoring broker's consent or without consent with at
least 24 hours' notice.  The licensee may have an attorney present.  The
Division's action will not be postponed due to a licensee's attorney's
unreasonable delay.  Escrow audits may include: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>1)         A review and examination of all required, original escrow
records as set forth in this Part. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>2)         A review and examination of any document, including physical
or electronic originals, related to a licensee's escrow accounts. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>3)         Interviews of any person, including any licensee or
non-licensee, who may have knowledge or information about the licensee's
practices for maintaining and administering escrow accounts. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>4)         The Division may require audits of special accounts of
sponsoring brokers by licensed certified public accountants as set forth in
this Section.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>A)        The Division shall notify in writing the sponsoring broker
responsible for the special accounts that an auditor has been retained to audit
the special accounts, the identity of the auditor or auditing firm, and that
the sponsoring broker shall submit all pertinent records for audit within 30
days after the written notice.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>B)        Any licensed certified public accountant performing an audit
for the Division pursuant to the provisions of this Section or the Act shall
provide a written report to the Division, with a copy to the sponsoring broker,
detailing the findings of the auditor, with specific reference to compliance
with the special account requirements of the Act and this Part.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>C)        The sponsoring broker shall be liable for the cost of the audit
if an order is issued by the Department pursuant to Section 20-60 of the Act
finding that escrow moneys were misappropriated by the sponsoring broker or the
sponsoring broker's employees, independent contractors, agents, or designees.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>c)         Pursuant to Section 5-45(d) of the Act, the Division is
authorized to obtain a licensee's original records, including physical or
electronic records, for the purposes of inspection, audit, and reproduction.  The
Division shall promptly return all original documents or records to the
licensee. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>(Source:  Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512, effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal><b> </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.925  Audits of Special Funds by Outside
Auditors (Repealed)</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>(Source: 
Repealed at 45 Ill. Reg. 2851, effective February 23, 2021)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.930  Case File Review Committee </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>a)         The Division
and the Board may appoint a Case File Review Committee that shall be composed
of at least 2 members of the Board, the Real Estate Coordinator, the Real
Estate Chief of Investigations and the Real Estate Chief of Prosecutions or
their designees.  The Case File Review Committee members may take action set
forth in this Section in person or through other means of communication.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>b)         The
Case File Review Committee may exercise the following duties and
responsibilities: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         Recommend
whether a complaint or case file be closed or refer the file to Investigations
or Prosecutions for further review and action. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         Review
reports and files submitted to the Case File Review Committee; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         Request
and review any investigation or prosecution files that the Department may have
closed. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>c)         The
Case File Review Committee shall report a summary of its actions and findings
at each Board meeting.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>d)         In
determining what action to recommend to the Department with regard to an
investigation and/or prosecution of a complaint or case file, the Case File
Review Committee shall consider factors including, but not limited to: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         The
effect on the public's health, safety, and welfare; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         Any
indication of fraud; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         Any
indication of commingling or embezzlement; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>4)         Evidence
of escrow account shortages or discrepancies; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>5)         Refusal
to provide escrow account records or related documents within the required time
period; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>6)         Prosecutorial
merit; or</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>7)         Any
alleged or potential violation of the Act or this Part.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>e)         Disqualification
of a Case File Review Committee member.  A Case File Review Committee member
shall be recused from consideration of a case file when conflict of interest,
or the appearance of a conflict of interest or bias or prejudice, would prevent
that Case File Review Committee member from being fair and impartial. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>f)         Participation
in the initial stages of the handling of a case file, including participation
on the Case File Review Committee and in informal conferences, shall not bar a
Case File Review Committee member from later participating in a formal hearing
or in deliberating the case and making a recommendation to the Director.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>g)         Any
meetings of the Case File Review Committee are an exception to the Open
Meetings Act [5 ILCS 120] and shall be closed to the public, in accordance with
Section 2(c)(15) of that Act. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>h)         Nothing
in this Section shall require the Department to utilize the services of the
Case File Review Committee to close any case file; however, the Department
shall be required, as set forth in this Section, to advise the Case File Review
Committee of such actions that are taken by the Department.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>(Source: 
Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512, effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b> </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.935  Peer Review
Advisor </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b> </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>a)         In accordance with Section 25-21 of the Act, the Department
may, in its discretion, contract with a licensee to act as an advisor to the
Department regarding public complaints and alleged violations forwarded by the
Department to the Peer Review Advisor.  </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>b)         The Department shall not be bound by any recommendation or
advice provided by a Peer Review Advisor. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>c)         The Department shall only refer matters to the Peer Review
Advisor that involve the actions of an unlicensed person or a licensee at the
same or lower level of licensure held by the Peer Review Advisor.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>d)         The Peer Review Advisor shall issue any recommendation or
findings to the Department in writing unless expressly waived by the
Department. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>(Source:  Amended at 40 Ill. Reg. 12588, effective September 2, 2016)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b> </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.940  Rules of
Practice in Administrative Hearings</b> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'>The Department of Financial and Professional
Regulation Rules of Practice in Administrative Hearings (68 Ill. Adm. Code 1110)
shall apply to all Department administrative hearings. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in;text-autospace:none'>(Source: 
Amended at 45 Ill. Reg. 2851, effective February 23, 2021)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.945  Real Estate
Recovery Fund</b> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>a)         Necessity of Notice </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-autospace:none'>When any
person obtains a judgment in a circuit court that may result in collection from
the Real Estate Recovery Fund, that person shall notify the Department of the
judgment in writing within 30 days after the entry of the judgment and shall
notify the Department in writing, within 20 days, notice of all supplementary
proceedings to allow the Department to intervene pursuant to Section 20-90. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>b)         Aggrieved Person</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-autospace:none'>"Aggrieved
person", as used in Sections 20-85, 20-90 and 20-105 of the Act, means a
person who has suffered harm or other injury as a result of the act or omission
of a licensee or an unlicensed employee of a licensee in connection with
licensed activities, including licensees who are a principal to the transaction
but are not providing licensed activities in the transaction, as set forth in
the Act.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>c)         Notice </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-autospace:none'>The notice
required by Section 20-90 of the Act or by this Section shall be sent by
certified mail, return receipt requested, by a recognized overnight courier
service, or shall be delivered by hand to the office of the Department in
Chicago, Illinois, Attention: General Counsel. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>d)         Contents of Notice </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-autospace:none'>Every notice
required by Section 20-90 of the Act or by this Section shall include: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>1)         Copies of the following documents provided by the aggrieved
parties: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>A)        The complaint and any amended complaints showing the
"Filed" stamp of the Clerk of the Court in which the complaint was
filed;  </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>B)        The judgment order and any post-judgment orders entered by the
Court; and </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>C)        Notice of all supplementary proceedings, which notice shall be
provided to the Department at least 20 days prior to any such proceedings.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>2)         Copies of relevant documents available to the claimant,
including: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>A)        Real estate sales contract, lease, closing statement,
disbursement directions or other evidence of title to real property on which
the claim is based.  If the claimant does not possess title, evidence of the
interest in real property on which the claim is based (evidence includes
documents such as title policy, deed, or lease); and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>B)        Proof of any check, money order, or other method of payment
regarding earnest money or security deposit, other negotiable instruments or
dishonored checks issued by the licensee. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>3)         An itemized statement of losses of actual cash money that the
claimant alleges occurred as a result of conduct identified in Section 20-85 of
the Act by any licensees or the unlicensed employee of a licensee.  When no
itemized statement is possible, the claimant must state under oath that the
claimant's losses are estimated, and the calculation of estimated losses is as
accurate as circumstances permit. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>e)         Party Defendant </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-autospace:none'>No notice of
claim will be recognized or accepted when the underlying complaint and
post-judgment order does not name at least one natural person or entity (a licensee,
its employees or independent contractors) as a defendant and judgment debtor. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>f)         Amounts of Recovery and Attorney's Fees</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-autospace:none'>By a
post-judgement order of the circuit court of the county where the violation
occurred in a proceeding described in Section 20-90 of the Act, the aggrieved
person may recover an amount of not more than $50,000 from the Recovery Fund
for damages sustained by any act, representation, transaction, or conduct
described in Section 20-85 of the Act, together with costs of suit and
attorney's fees in connection with the conduct leading to the claim.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-autospace:none'>1)         The
post-judgement order shall not include interest.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>2)         The maximum liability of damages arising out of the activities
of any one licensee or one unlicensed employee of a licensee in any transaction
or set of facts that formed the basis of a post-judgement order (including
costs of suit and attorney's fees, when an attorney's affidavit as to their
fees is sufficient proof) is $300,000.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>3)         The amount of any claim paid from the Fund will be spread
equitably among all co-owners or otherwise aggrieved persons, if any.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>(Source:  Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512, effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b> </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.950  Automatic Revocation
Upon Order for Payment from the Real Estate Recovery Fund (Repealed)</b> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in;text-autospace:none'>(Source: 
Repealed at 45 Ill. Reg. 2851, effective February 23, 2021)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal><b> </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.955  Nonbinding Advisory Opinions (Repealed)</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>(Source:  Repealed
at 45 Ill. Reg. 2851, effective February 23, 2021)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.960  Citations for Non-Compliance with
Continuing Education and Post-License Education Requirements</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>
