<p class=MsoNormal>The Division shall conduct audits to verify compliance with
the CE and post-license education requirements of the Act and this Part.  If,
during an audit or compliance review, the Division determines that a licensee
may be deficient in complying with CE or post-license education requirements,
the Division will issue a citation to the licensee of the deficiency.  Service
of the citation shall be made in person, electronically or by mail to the
licensee at the licensee's address or email address of record.  The licensee
shall have 60 days after the date of service of the citation to submit evidence
of compliance with CE requirements to the Division and to pay the fine imposed.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>a)         The
citation shall inform the licensee that they may provide the Division with
proof that the licensee has completed the required CE or post-license education
prior to the renewal deadline or deadlines associated with the audit.  If
satisfactory proof is provided within 60 days after the citation is served, the
citation shall become null and void.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>b)         The
citation shall set forth the fine imposed and inform the licensee that the
licensee may request a hearing contesting the deficiency within 30 days after
the citation is served.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>c)         The
citation shall be non-public and become a final non-disciplinary order if the
cited licensee does not request a hearing within 30 days after the citation is
served or if the cited licensee does not provide the Division with proof of
completing the required CE or post-license education, prior to the renewal
deadline or deadlines associated with the audit.  The fine shall be payable
within 30 days after the date of the final order.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>d)         A fine
in the amount of $500 shall be assessed for a first citation for noncompliance
with CE or post-license education requirements.  A fine in the amount of $1,000
shall be assessed for a second citation for noncompliance with CE or
post-license education requirements.  Pursuant to Section 20-20.1(c) of the
Act, a third and each subsequent citation for noncompliance with CE or
post-license education requirements shall result in publicly disclosed discipline
and a fine in an amount not to exceed $2,000.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>e)         Payment
of the fine does not absolve the person from the responsibility to complete the
CE or post-license education requirements and correct the violation.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>f)         If
the licensee fails to submit satisfactory evidence of compliance with CE or
post-license education requirements and to pay the fine imposed pursuant to the
final order, the Department may take further disciplinary action pursuant to
the Act and this Part for violating the terms of the order.  The licensee shall
not engage in real estate activities for which a license is required under the
Act until all required CE has been completed and payment is made for the fine
imposed by the citation and for any fees required to reinstate or restore the
license.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in'>(Source:  Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512,
effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>
<br><p class="subheading">SUBPART J:  GRANTING VARIANCES</p>

<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.1000  Granting
Variances</b> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'>The Director may grant variances
from this Part in cases in which the Director finds that: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>a)         The provision from which the variance is granted is not
statutorily mandated; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>b)         No party will be injured by the granting of the variance; and </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>c)         Application of the rule from which the variance is granted
would, in the particular case, be unreasonable or unnecessarily burdensome. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>(Source:  Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512, effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>
<br><p class="subheading">SUBPART K:  EDUCATION PROVIDERS, INSTRUCTORS, AND COURSE APPROVAL</p>

<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.1100  Application for Education Provider
License and Other Requirements</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>a)         A
person or entity seeking to provide pre-license education, the 45-hour
post-license education, or CE shall submit: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         A
signed and completed education provider's application in a format provided by
the Division;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         The required
fee set forth in Section 1450.130; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         A
course description, learning objectives, comprehensive timed outline and, when applicable,
the final examination, answer key for each course, and final exam proctor
policy; and any other information required by the Division.  Education
providers shall be responsible for any substantial misrepresentations made by
the provider concerning the subject matter of a course;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>4)         Applications
and fees for each course, as set forth in Sections 1450.1105(a) and
1450.1135(a); and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>5)         Authorization
to conduct or transact business in Illinois issued by the SOS.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>b)         The
Division shall notify the applicant in writing of its decision.  In the event
the Department intends to deny the application, then it shall include a clear
statement of the acts or omissions alleged to violate the Act or this Part. 
The notice shall notify the applicant that they must request a hearing to contest
the notice within 30 days after service and that if a request is not filed
within that time, the Director may issue an order denying the license
application (see Section 20-60 of the Act and Article 10 of the Illinois
Administrative Procedure Act [5 ILCS 100]).</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>c)         The education program offered
by an education provider shall: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         Be
approved by the education provider's governing and/or supervising body; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         Only
use instructors with a valid Illinois instructor license issued under the Act
and Sections 1450.1115 and 1450.1145; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         Have
a curriculum that meets the requirements of Section 1450.1105 and/or Section 1450.1135;
and </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>4)         Include
a proctored final examination, if a proctored examination is required, as
outlined in Sections 1450.1105 and 1450.1135.  Any remote proctoring must be
conducted in accordance with the requirements of state and federal law.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>d)         Records and Facilities </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:108.3pt;text-indent:-36.3pt'>1)         Education
providers must maintain all records, including, but not limited to,
transcripts, attendance records, and certificates of completion, for a period
of not less than 5 years regardless of whether the education provider's license
is active.  Those records may be maintained in physical or electronic form.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:108.3pt;text-indent:-36.3pt'>2)         Education
providers must provide customer service email or telephone contact information.
</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:108.3pt;text-indent:-36.3pt'>3)         Education
providers and their records are subject to inspection at any time after
submitting an application to the Division. The inspection shall be made during
regular business hours, with at least 24 hours advance notice of the
inspection. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:108.3pt;text-indent:-36.3pt'>4)         No
education provider shall conduct in-person business in a private residence or
retail establishment unless the education provider's business location within
the retail establishment is a separate and distinct space solely occupied by
the education provider. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:108.3pt;text-indent:-36.3pt'>5)         Whenever
an education provider intends to operate at a location other than the location
of record, the education provider shall, no later than 10 days prior to that operation,
notify the Division in a format provided by the Division. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:108.3pt;text-indent:-36.3pt'>6)         Education
providers may establish and operate virtual offices that do not have a fixed
location.  If the licensee conducts education provider services in or from the
virtual office, the virtual office shall:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>A)        Display
a current registry of all licensed instructors, courses, and their
corresponding license numbers;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>B)        Display
contact information for the education provider's owner or operator, including
name, license number, valid phone number and email address;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>C)        Maintain
a secure intranet or secure portal that is accessible by employees,
instructors, students, and, upon request, the Division.  The intranet portal
shall include:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>i)          Electronic
records, securely stored in the format they were originally created, sent, or
received, and made accessible for inspection by the Division in a secure
electronic format.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>ii)         Documents
originally created in physical form may be converted to an electronic format
only if the content is not altered.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>D)        Service
of Process. For those education providers who maintain a virtual office, a
valid physical address in Illinois must be provided to the Department for
service of process.  If an education provider does not maintain a physical
address in Illinois, they may utilize the same address provided to the SOS for
its registered agent in Illinois.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:108.3pt;text-indent:-36.3pt'>7)         If
an education provider and brokerage are under the same ownership structure or
are affiliated entities, each shall be separately licensed with the Division. 
The education provider and brokerage's business operations must function
independently and be distinct from one another.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>e)         Administration </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         No education
provider shall advertise that it is endorsed, recommended or accredited by the
Division.  The education provider may indicate that the school is licensed by
the Division. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         Every
education provider shall submit to the Division, upon the Division's request, a
schedule including location, date, time and name of each licensed pre-license or
CE instructor for each licensed pre-license, post-license or CE course offered.
The education provider shall, upon the Division's request, notify the Division
of any changes to that schedule. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><span style='color:black'> </span></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'><span
style='color:black'>3)         Every education provider shall specify, in any
advertising promoting pre-license, post-license or CE courses, the number of
pre-license, post-license or CE credit hours that may be earned toward Illinois
pre-license, post-license or CE requirements.</span></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>4)         Prior
to enrollment, the education provider shall provide a prospective student with
information that specifies:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in'>A)        The course of study to
be offered and the delivery method;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in'>B)        The tuition to be
charged;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>C)        The education
provider's policy regarding refund of unearned tuition when a student is
dismissed or withdraws voluntarily or through hardship;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>D)        Any
additional fee to be charged for supplies, materials or books that become the
property of the student upon payment; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>E)        Other
matters that are material to the relationship between the education provider
and the student. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>5)         Each education
provider shall maintain for each student a record including the course of
instruction undertaken, dates of attendance, areas of study satisfactorily
completed, and, if applicable, the date on which the final exam was
successfully passed.  Each student's record shall be maintained by the education
provider, regardless of whether the education provider's license is currently
active, for a period of 5 years and shall be available to the student or for
inspection by the Division during regular business hours.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>6)         An
education provider shall issue a transcript or certificate of completion, in a format
provided by the Division, upon a student's successful completion of any
course.  Each transcript or certificate of completion shall be authenticated
and capable of being verified by the education provider.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>7)         The education
provider shall certify on the initial application that sufficient financial
resources are available to equip and maintain the school.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'><span
style='color:black'>8)         The education provider shall verify attendance
at each pre-license course, post-license course, and CE course.  A student may
only receive credit for time actually spent attending the course or, if applicable,
for achieving a passing score of at least 75% on the final examination to
receive credit.</span></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'><span
style='color:black'>9)         Each education provider shall provide time and facilities
for conducting make-up classes for students absent from the regularly scheduled
class.  No more than 10% of the total credit hours may include make-up credit
hours.  A student missing any make-up credit hours shall fail the course.</span></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-40.95pt'>10)         The
Division shall be reimbursed by any out-of-state education provider for all
reasonable expenses incurred by an inspector to inspect its facilities or
records.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-40.95pt'>11)         An
education provider shall submit proof of ownership as may, from time to time,
be required by the Division.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-40.95pt'>12)         Course
Completion Records.  Every licensed education provider shall submit to the
Division, in a format provided by the Division, on or before the 15<sup>th</sup>
of each month, a report of applicants or licensees passing education courses
offered by the education provider during the preceding calendar month.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>A)        An
administrative fee in the amount of $50 will be imposed on an education
provider that fails to submit its monthly report on or before the 15<sup>th</sup>
of the month. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>B)        If an
education provider fails to submit monthly reports and/or to pay the required
late fees for three or more successive months, the license of the education
provider and the courses offered by that education provider may be subject to
discipline as set forth in Section 1450.1165.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>f)         Education
providers offering a 30-hour real estate auction certification course must
comply with Section 1450.1300.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>g)         Each
university, college, community college, or school that is a public institution
of higher education, supported by public funds, and located in the State of
Illinois shall be deemed to qualify as an education provider, as long as that
university, college, community college, or school meets the following criteria
and certifies to the Division that:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         The
facility is domiciled in the State of Illinois and supported by public funds;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:.5in'>2)         The
instructors are approved and licensed by the Division;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         The
courses offered are approved and licensed by the Division;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>4)         The
program that offers pre-license or continuing education is a not-for-profit
division of the university, college, community college, or school; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>5)         The
courses and curriculum meet the requirements of Section 1450.1105.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>(Source:  Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512,
effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.1105  Application for Pre-License and Post-License
Courses </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>a)         A
licensed education provider seeking to provide pre-license courses shall submit
for each course:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         A
signed and completed pre-license course application in a format provided by the
Division;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         The
fee required by Section 1450.130; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         A
course description, comprehensive timed outline, and, when applicable, final examination,
answer key for each course, final exam proctor policy, and any other
information required by the Division.  Each outline shall make reference to the
textbook used and other material related to the course or subject matter and
shall conform to the standardized curriculum provided by the Division.  The
applicant shall make any education material referenced in the outline available
to the Division, upon request.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>b)         An education
provider offering pre-license courses must provide the following pre-license broker
courses as recommended by the Board and approved by the Division:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         A 60
credit hour course including, but not limited to, the following topics:
Introduction to License Law, Real Property, Current State and Federal Law, Real
Estate Transactions, and Real Estate Career Paths. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         A 15
credit hour Applied Real Estate Principles course, presented in the classroom,
by interactive webinar, or by online distance education.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-34.6pt'>c)         An
education provider offering pre-license courses must provide the following 45-hour
post-license broker courses:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         A 15
credit hour course on Applied Brokerage Principles, recommended by the Board
and approved by the Division, and presented in the classroom, by interactive
webinar, or by online distance education.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         A 15
credit hour course on Risk Management/Discipline, recommended by the Board and
approved by the Division, and presented in the classroom, by interactive webinar,
or by online distance education.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         A 15
credit hour course on Transactional Issues, recommended by the Board and
approved by the Division, and presented in the classroom, by interactive
webinar, or by online distance education.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>4)         Each
15 credit hour post-license course must be followed by a 50 question
examination administered by the education provider, for which the minimum
passing score shall be no less than 75%.  Education providers offering
classroom-based courses may administer the exam online or in an electronic
format.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>d)         An education
provider offering pre-license courses must provide the following pre-license managing
broker courses, recommended by the Board, and approved by the Division:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         A 30
credit hour course including, but not limited to, Brokerage Administration and
Management, Licensing, Escrow and Residential Leasing Agent Management.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         A 15
credit hour Applied Management and Supervision course, presented in the
classroom, by interactive webinar, or by online distance education.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>e)         Only
those education providers that provide courses enumerated in subsections (b),
(c) and (d) may provide CE core classes or the 30-hour pre-license endorsement
courses.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>f)         A
licensed real estate education provider may provide a 15 credit hour course as
set forth in Section 5-10(a)(5) of the Act for pre-license residential leasing
agents. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>g)         A
licensed real estate education provider may provide a 30 credit hour real
estate auction certification course as set forth in Section 5-32(b) of the Act for
real estate auctioneers.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:71.9pt;text-indent:-34.5pt'>h)        Examinations. 
Each correspondence or home study pre-license course shall require a mandatory
proctored final examination (see Section 30-15(i)(6) of the Act) prepared and
provided by the licensed education provider and consisting of at least 25
questions for every 15 credit hours. Any remote proctoring must be conducted in
accordance with the requirements of state and federal law.  The minimum passing
score shall be no less than 75%. The proctored final examination shall be
provided by the education provider at the completion of every 15 credit hours
or the conclusion of the course. The education provider's registration material
shall inform the student in writing whether the proctored final examination
will be administered in electronic format, paper format, or both.  Credit hours
exclude any time devoted to taking the proctored final examination.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>i)          A
licensed education provider must verify attendance for courses presented in a
classroom, by interactive webinar, or by online distance education.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>j)          The Division
may approve courses that incorporate additional real estate topics. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>(Source: 
Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512, effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal><b> </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.1110  Application for Education Provider and
Course License Renewal  </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b> </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>a)         Education
provider and course licenses shall expire on June 30 of each odd-numbered year.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>b)         Licensed
education providers shall be responsible for submitting:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         A
renewal application in a format provided by the Division;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>2)         The required fee set
forth in Section 1450.130; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         A
list of updated courses, course descriptions, comprehensive timed outlines, proctored
final examinations, answer keys, and any other information required by the Division.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>c)         Failure
to receive a renewal notice or failure to pay the renewal fee shall not
constitute valid grounds for failing to renew a license.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>d)         Operating
on an expired education provider license shall constitute unlicensed practice
and may be grounds for discipline.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>e)         An education
provider license expired for more than 2 years shall not be eligible for
renewal.  An education provider license expired for less than 2 years may be
renewed after the education provider submits evidence that all requirements of
Section 1450.1100 have been met and the fees required by Section 1450.130 have
been paid.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>(Source: 
Amended at 45 Ill. Reg. 2851, effective February 23, 2021)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.1115  Application for Pre-License Instructor
License </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>a)         A
person seeking a pre-license instructor license shall meet the following
requirements: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         Pass
an examination, authorized by the Division, with a minimum score of 75%;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         Demonstrate
expertise by:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>A)        Holding
an active broker or managing broker license for the immediately preceding 2
years; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>B)        Being
admitted to the practice of law by the Supreme Court of Illinois;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>C)        Having
prior teaching experience for a minimum period of 2 years; or</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>D)        Professional
background and experience.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'><span
style='font-family:"Times New \(W1\)"'>3)         Complete</span> a 12-hour
instructor training program approved by the Division and comprised of: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>A)        6
hours devoted to instructor training and development; and </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>B)        6
hours devoted to teaching the content of the pre-license courses set forth in
Section 1450.1105.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>b)         The
Division shall notify the applicant in writing of its decision.  The Division
may restrict a pre-license instructor's license to teaching only certain
courses or certain types of courses.  In the event the application is denied,
the basis for denial will be provided and the applicant advised that the
applicant may request a hearing on the merits, as set forth in Section 20-60 of
the Act and Article 10 of the Illinois Administrative Procedure Act.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>c)         A
licensed pre-license instructor may teach pre-license, post-license, CE core or
elective curriculum, or broker management CE curriculum without obtaining a CE
instructor license.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>(Source: 
Amended at 45 Ill. Reg. 2851, effective February 23, 2021)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal><b> </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.1120  Administration of Proficiency
Examinations and Eligibility to Take the Proficiency Exam and Transition
Courses (Repealed)</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>(Source: 
Repealed at 40 Ill. Reg. 12588, effective September 2, 2016)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b><i> </i></b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><a name="OLE_LINK25"></a><a
name="OLE_LINK26"><b>Section 1450.1125  Pre-License Instructor License Renewal
and Restoration</b> </a></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>a)         Pre-license instructor licenses shall expire on June 30 of
each odd-numbered year. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>b)         Licensed pre-license instructors shall be responsible for
submitting:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>1)         A renewal application in a format provided by the Division;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>2)         The required fee set forth in Section 1450.130; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>3)         Certification of:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-autospace:none'>A)        Maintaining
a valid pre-license instructor's license;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>B)        No lapse in licensure greater than 2 years; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>C)        One of the following:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>i)          Teaching at least one pre-license, post-license, broker
management CE or core CE course during the current term of licensure; or</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>ii)         Successful completion, during the current term of licensure,
of the 12-hour instructor training program set forth in Section
1450.1115(a)(3).  The 12-hour instructor training program shall not count
towards the required residential leasing agent, broker or managing broker CE
requirements. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>c)         Failure to receive a renewal notice or failure to pay the
renewal fee shall not constitute valid grounds for failing to renew a license. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>d)         Teaching courses on an expired pre-license instructor license
shall constitute unlicensed practice and may be grounds for discipline. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>e)         Restoration</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>1)         A pre-license instructor with an expired license may renew the
license without paying any lapsed renewal fees provided that the license
expired while the pre-license instructor was: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>A)        On active duty with the United States Army, United States Navy,
United States Marine Corps, United States Air Force, United States Coast Guard
or State Militia called into the service or training for the United States; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>B)        Engaged in training or education under the supervision of the United States prior to induction into military service; or </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>C)        Serving as an employee of the Department. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         A
pre-license instructor renewing a license as set forth in this subsection (e)
may renew the license within a period of 2 years following the termination of
service and is not required to reapply or complete any examination or
instructor training to renew. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>f)         Except as set forth in this Section, any pre-license
instructor whose license has expired for more than 2 years shall meet the new
applicant requirements as set forth in Section 1450.1115. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>(Source: 
Amended at 45 Ill. Reg. 2851, effective February 23, 2021)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.1130  Application
for Continuing Education School License and Other Requirements (Repealed)</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>(Source:  Repealed
at 43 Ill. Reg. 1975, effective January 25, 2019)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.1135  Application for Continuing Education
Courses and Curriculum </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>a)         A
licensed education provider seeking to provide CE courses shall submit:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         A
signed and completed CE course application in a format provided by the
Division;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in'>2)         The required fee set
forth in Section 1450.130; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         A
course description, learning objectives, comprehensive timed outline, course
objectives and, when required, the final examination and answer key for each
course, as well as the exam proctor policy and written proctor agreement.
Materials  provided to the students and other information may also be required
by the Division.  Each outline shall make reference to the textbook used, if
applicable, and other material related to the course or subject matter and
shall conform to a standardized curriculum approved by the Division. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>b)         CE
courses shall comply with a standardized curriculum provided by the Division.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>c)         A
licensee may earn credit for a specific CE course only once during the current
term of the license.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>d)         Each CE course shall include one or more subjects from either
the core category, as set forth in Sections 1450.450(b)(3)(A) and 1450.540(b)(3)(A),
or elective category, as set forth in Sections 1450.450(b)(3)(B) and
1450.540(b)(3)(B), regardless of whether students are in a classroom or are being
taught by an interactive webinar or in an online distance education format.  All
CE courses shall be a minimum of 1 credit hour and shall be offered, at a
minimum, in 1 hour increments.  The education provider shall clearly indicate
on the certificate of completion the number of credit hours earned from each CE
course and identify whether the course was from the core or elective category. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in;text-autospace:none'>e)         All
CE courses shall: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>1)         Contribute to the advancement, integrity, extension and
enhancement of professional skills and knowledge in the practice of licensed
activities; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>2)         Provide experiences that contain subject matter and course
materials relevant to that set forth in Section 5-70 of the Act.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>f)         Each CE course, except those offered in a classroom, by interactive
webinar, or by online distance education, shall end in a mandatory proctored final
exam prepared and provided by the licensed education provider consisting of at
least 12 questions for every 1 credit hour and for which the minimum passing
score shall be no less than 75%.  The education provider's registration
material shall inform the student in writing if the examination will be in electronic
format,  paper format, or both.  Credit hours exclude any time devoted to
taking the examination. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>1)         Pursuant to Section 5-70(j) of the Act, no more than 12 hours
of CE credit may be taken in one calendar day.  The proctored final exam for a
CE course or courses may be given at the end of each individual course or group
of courses.  For example, a licensee who intends to take 12 hours of CE may
complete the proctored final exam and earn CE credit for the courses at the end
of each individual course or group of courses, provided that the licensee does
not exceed the 12-hour limit of instruction per calendar day referenced in
Section 5-70(j) of the Act. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>2)         All examinations shall be proctored pursuant to an exam
proctor policy.  Proctors must be impartial third parties (i.e., not a
licensee's sponsoring broker, managing broker, relative, or colleague) as
defined in Section 1450.100.  Any remote proctoring must be conducted in
accordance with the requirements of State and federal law.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>3)         If a student fails a CE examination twice, the student must
successfully retake the CE course and pass the examination in order to receive
credit. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>4)         The education provider shall provide a closed book examination
when required.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>g)         A licensed education provider must verify student attendance
for all courses presented in a classroom, by interactive webinar, or by online
distance education.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>(Source:  Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512, effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.1137  Authorization for Third Party Designees
to Review Courses</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>a)         Pursuant
to Section 30-15(j) of the Act, the Department may retain a third party to act
as the Board's designee to review course materials submitted for approval.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>b)         The
Department may consider the recommendation of the Board on the engagement of
the third party.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in'>(Source:  Amended at 45 Ill. Reg. 2851,
effective February 23, 2021)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.1140  Application for Continuing Education School
and Course License Renewal (Repealed)</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>(Source: 
Repealed at 43 Ill. Reg. 1975, effective January 25, 2019)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.1145  Application for Continuing Education Instructor
License</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>a)         A
person seeking a CE instructor license shall submit:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         A signed
and completed application in a format provided by the Division;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         The required
fee set forth in Section 1450.130; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         A
certification that the applicant has attended and successfully completed a 6-hour
instructor development course.  This course may be the same as the 6-hour
instructor training and development course set forth in Section 1450.1115(a)(3)(A). 
The 6-hour instructor training and development course shall not count towards residential
leasing agent, broker, managing broker CE or pre-license instructor renewal
requirements.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>b)         Applicants
for a CE instructor license shall meet at least one of the following criteria:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         Be licensed
and active as a broker or managing broker for the immediately preceding 3
years; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         Be
currently admitted to practice law and, for the immediately preceding 3 years, have
been engaged in real estate related work as part of the active practice of law,
or taught real estate pre-license courses;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         Be a
credentialed real estate course instructor engaged in teaching for the
immediately preceding 3 years; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>4)         Hold a
professional designation, including, but not limited to, a designated real
estate instructor (DREI); </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>5)         Be approved
by a college or university to teach a real estate degree program;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>6)         Have
been licensed or certified to engage in the business of certified real estate
appraiser, certified mortgage loan originator, home inspector, auctioneer, or
certified public accountant, or other related profession as established by the Division
for the immediately preceding 3 years; or</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>7)         Be
qualified by experience or education as set forth in Section 30-25 of the Act.
In determining whether a person is qualified to teach CE under that Section,
the Division may consider:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in'>A)        Teaching experience; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in'>B)        Real estate experience; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in'>C)        Any real estate,
business, or legal education. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>c)         The
Division shall notify the applicant in writing of its decision.  The Division
may restrict a CE instructor's license to teaching only certain CE courses or
certain types of CE courses. In the event the application is denied, the
reasons will be provided, and the applicant advised that the applicant may
request a hearing on the merits pursuant to Section 20-60 of the Act and
Article 10 of the Illinois Administrative Procedure Act.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>d)         A CE instructor
who holds a CE instructor license may only teach CE elective courses.  If the
CE instructor wishes to teach any CE core curriculum or broker management CE courses,
the instructor must also hold a valid pre-license instructor license.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>(Source: 
Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512, effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.1150  Continuing
Education Instructor License Renewal and Restoration</b> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>a)         CE instructor licenses shall expire on June 30 of each odd-numbered
year. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>b)         Licensed CE instructors shall be responsible for submitting:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>1)         A renewal application in a format provided by the Division;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>2)         The required fee set forth in Section 1450.130; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>3)         One of the following:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>A)        Certification of having taught at least 1 elective CE course
during the current term of licensure; or</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>B)        Successful completion, during the current term of licensure, of
the 6-hour instructor training and development course set forth in Section
1450.1145(a).</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>c)         Failure to receive a renewal notice or failure to pay the
renewal fee shall not constitute  valid grounds for failing to renew a license.
</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>d)         Teaching CE elective courses on an expired license shall
constitute unlicensed practice and may be grounds for discipline or
non-disciplinary action. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>e)         Restoration</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>1)         A CE instructor with an expired license may renew the license
without paying any lapsed renewal fees provided that the CE license expired
while the instructor was: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>A)        On active duty with the United States Army, United States Navy,
United States Marine Corps, United States Air Force, United States Coast Guard
or State Militia called into the service or training for the United States; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>B)        Engaged in training or education under the supervision of the United States prior to induction into military service; or </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>C)        Serving as the Director or as an employee of the Department. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>2)         A CE instructor renewing a license as set forth in this
subsection (e) may renew the license within a period of 2 years following the
termination of service and is not required to reapply or complete any
examination or instructor training to renew. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>f)         Except as set forth in this Section, any CE instructor whose
license is expired for more than 2 years shall meet the new applicant
requirements set forth in Section 1450.1145.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal><span style='font-family:"Times New \(W1\)"'> </span></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>(Source: 
Amended at 45 Ill. Reg. 2851, effective February 23, 2021)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.1155  Correspondence
or Home Study Courses </b> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal>The Division may consider the recommendation of the Board
regarding correspondence or home study course curriculum.  Correspondence or
home study courses are non-interactive courses in which students review and
learn material through self-study, without any mandatory interaction with a
licensed instructor, and in which participation and attendance cannot be
verified.  Correspondence or home study courses shall not include classroom
instruction.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>a)         The following may not be taught as correspondence or home
study courses:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         Core
CE (see Section 30-15(i)(2) of the Act);</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         The
15-hour Broker Pre-license Applied Real Estate Principles course (see Section
5-27(a)(5) of the Act and Section 1450.1105(b)(2)); </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         The
45-hour broker post-license education courses (see Section 5-50(b) of the Act);
</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>4)         The
12-hour broker management CE course (see Section 5-70(b) of the Act); </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>5)         The
15-hour Managing Broker Pre-license Applied Management and Supervision course (see
Section 5-28(a)(5) of the Act and Section 1450.1105(d)(2)); and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>6)         Sexual
Harassment Prevention Training (see Section 2105-15.5 of the Department of
Professional Regulation Law [20 ILCS 2105]).</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>b)         Correspondence or home study courses shall be provided by a licensed
education provider and meet the requirements set forth in Sections 1450.1100 and
1450.1105, as applicable, and any additional requirements established by the
Act and this Part, except that:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>1)         The licensed education provider shall be responsible for
ensuring that the proctor verifies attendance at the proctored final examination.
 The identity of each student must be verified by valid and unexpired photo identification
(i.e., driver's license, state identification card, passport, etc.) prior to
the start of the examination.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>2)         The examination site for correspondence or home study shall be
determined by the education provider and shall be proctored by a representative
of the education provider or by means of electronic proctoring. A licensed
instructor is not required to proctor an examination. Proctors must be
impartial third parties (i.e., not a licensee's sponsoring broker, managing
broker, relative, or colleague).</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>c)         Students must be provided with complete information on the
nature and the content of the correspondence or home study course.<span
style='font-size:9.0pt;font-family:"Verdana",sans-serif;color:black;background:
white'> </span>A student enrolled in a correspondence or home study course
shall receive the following prior to beginning the course, as applicable:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>1)         Education provider and instructor contact information (e.g., telephone,
email, business address);</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>2)         Homework assignments;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>3)         Testing information (e.g., sites, proctors);</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>4)         Schedules and deadlines;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>5)         List of student materials and resources required (e.g.,
written materials, software);</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>6)         Grading and course credit information;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>7)         Resource information;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>8)         Registration and withdrawal periods;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>9)         Fees;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-42.75pt;text-autospace:
none'>10)         Americans with Disabilities Act (42 USC 12101 et seq.) information,
including special needs provisions;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-41.85pt;text-autospace:
none'>11)         Delivery procedures;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-41.85pt;text-autospace:
none'>12)         Technology support services available to students; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-41.85pt;text-autospace:
none'>13)         The procedure for issuing certificates of completion.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>d)         No continuing education credit shall be awarded or earned for
a correspondence or home study course that fails to meet the proctored final
examination requirements (see Section 30-15(i)(6)).  </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>e)         Licensed education providers providing correspondence or home
study courses shall establish written procedures for proctoring and grading
examinations. There shall also be written objectives for each course and written
procedures for the prompt return of materials, if required.  Copies of these procedures
shall be provided to the Division upon application.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><span style='font-family:"Times New \(W1\)"'> </span></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'><span style='font-family:"Times New \(W1\)"'>f</span>)         Licensed education
providers offering correspondence or home study courses shall provide for a
licensed instructor and technical support to be available during normal
business hours to answer student questions. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>g)         Correspondence or home study courses shall require at least the
same amount of time to complete as a classroom course.  For each correspondence
or home study course, an education provider shall include in its initial
application a comprehensive timed outline consistent with course hour
requirements.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>h)         Licensed
education providers shall not administer a correspondence or home study course proctored
final examination to a student until the student has had adequate time to
complete the course.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in'>(Source:  Amended at 45 Ill. Reg. 2851,
effective February 23, 2021)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b> </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.1160 
Recruitment</b> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>a)         Licensees shall not recruit exam takers to become associated
with a licensee at test facilities where the Illinois Real Estate Licensing Examinations
are conducted. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>b)         No licensed education providers shall allow the education provider's
premises or classrooms to be used during class time by anyone to directly or
indirectly recruit students to become associated with a licensee.  Education providers
and instructors shall promptly report any efforts to recruit students during
class time to the Division.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>c)         The education provider and the testing vendor are prohibited
from disclosing information about any student to anyone other than the
Division, except as may be necessary for purposes of completing application or
testing related and ministerial matters strictly for the benefit of the
student.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>(Source:  Amended at 45 Ill. Reg. 2851, effective February 23, 2021)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.1165  Discipline
of Education Providers, Instructors</b><b> and Courses</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>a)         Upon written recommendation of the Board to the Director, the Department
may refuse to issue or renew a license, reprimand, fine, place on probation,
suspend or revoke any license or otherwise discipline any license of any education
provider, pre-license instructor, pre-license course, CE instructor, CE course,
post-license course, or an applicant for any license when: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>1)         The quality of the course, instruction or program fails to
meet the established criteria as set forth in the Act and this Part; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>2)         The licensee commits fraud or misrepresentation in applying
for a license; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>3)         Any other professional license, accreditation or certification
of the instructor, school or course is suspended, revoked, or otherwise
disciplined; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>4)         The licensee dismisses a student from a course, without good
cause, and that dismissal results in required hours not being met;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:.5in;text-autospace:
none'>5)         The licensee fails to adhere to approved course materials;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>6)         The licensee conducts a course while the license is inactive, nonrenewed,
expired, suspended, revoked, or surrendered, or an applicant conducts a course prior
to being issued a license;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>7)         A licensee plagiarizes course material of another;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>8)         A course is not conducted in accordance with the delivery
method represented to the Division at the time the application for licensure
was submitted, or a course no longer complies with the criteria for the licensure;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>9)         The licensee does not enforce policies relating to courses,
instructor qualifications, student attendance, or course scheduling;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-42.75pt;text-autospace:
none'>10)         The licensee or applicant misrepresents any material fact
relating to a course;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-42.75pt;text-autospace:
none'>11)         The licensee fails to maintain, for a period of at least 5
years, accurate records of students' course completion or fulfill, within 14
days, student, or Division requests for course completion certificates;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-42.75pt;text-autospace:
none'>12)         The licensee assists a student, directly or indirectly, in
cheating on an examination, including, but not limited to, providing a copy of
the exam or questions and answers to the exam; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-42.75pt;text-autospace:
none'>13)         The licensee or applicant makes any substantial
misrepresentation, or engages in misleading or untruthful advertising,
including, but not limited to, guaranteeing success or a "passing
score" on any examination or any course, or using any trade name or indicia
of membership in any organization in which the applicant or licensee is not a
member; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-42.75pt;text-autospace:
none'>14)         The licensee or applicant teaches courses without being
licensed, assists an unlicensed individual in teaching a course, or teaches an
unlicensed or unapproved course; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-42.75pt;text-autospace:
none'>15)         The licensee or applicant fails to provide information to the
Division as required under any provision of the Act or this Part; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-42.75pt;text-autospace:
none'>16)         The licensee or applicant disregards or violates any
provision of the Act or this Part; or</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-42.75pt;text-autospace:
none'>17)         A licensed pre-license or CE instructor fails to notify an education
provider that the instructor license is subject to a restriction set forth in
Sections 1450.1115(b) and 1450.1145(d).</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-42.75pt;text-autospace:
none'>18)         The licensed instructor provides materially false or
misleading information concerning the subject of the course.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>b)         Disciplinary proceedings shall be conducted as set forth in
the Act and this Part. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>(Source:  Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512, effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b> </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.1170  Real
Estate Education Advisory Council (Repealed)</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in;text-autospace:none'>(Source: 
Repealed at 43 Ill. Reg. 1975, effective January 25, 2019)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.1175  Waiver of Final Examination
Requirements for Certain Education Delivery Methods</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>a)         The option
of not requiring a final examination (see Section 30-15(i)(5) of the Act) shall
apply to CE and pre-license courses.  This option shall not apply to the 45-hour
broker post-license education (see Section 5-50(b) of the Act).</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>b)         To
qualify for the final examination exemption under Section 30-15(i)(5) of the
Act, an education provider shall provide evidence, at the Division's request<a
name="_Hlk504737166">, </a>that all students in a course offered in a classroom
participate by:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         Recording
each student's attendance at the beginning of the class and at the end of each
class; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         Retaining
a record, either physically or electronically, of each student's participation,
for a minimum of 5 years.  Records required by this subsection (b) are subject
to audit by the Division after 24 hours' notice. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>c)         To
qualify for the final examination exemption under Section 30-15(i)(5) of the
Act, an education provider shall, at the Division's request, provide evidence
that all students in an interactive webinar course demonstrate their
participation by recording each student's attendance once at the beginning of
the class and at least once per 50 minutes of instruction (see Section
30-15(i)(3) of the Act).  Students shall demonstrate their attendance by
answering or responding to polling questions initiated by the instructor.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>d)         To
qualify for the final examination exemption under Section 30-15(i)(5) of the
Act, an education provider shall, at the Division's request, provide evidence
that all students in an online distance education course demonstrated their
participation and comprehension by:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>1)         Requiring
and recording each student's responses and answers and verifying that the
student satisfied the 75% minimum comprehension threshold. No more than 250
questions shall be required in any one course; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>2)         Retaining
each student's responses and answers, either physically or electronically, for
a minimum of 5 years.  The records are subject to audit by the Division after
24 hours' notice;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>3)         Providing
that, should a student fail to meet the 75% minimum comprehension requirement,
the student will be given another opportunity to demonstrate comprehension
within the same course, provided that the questions, response choices, and
answers are randomized and not in the same order as first presented to the
student; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>4)         Verifying
a student's identity at the beginning and conclusion of the course and maintaining
that verification with date and time stamp, either physically or electronically,
for 5 years.  The records are subject to audit by the Division after 24 hours'
notice.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>e)         Education
providers, upon request of the Division, shall provide records of student
attendance and/or performance.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>(Source: 
Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512, effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.1180   Proctor Standards</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>
