<p class=MsoNormal>The proctor shall authenticate the identity of the student
taking the examination; monitor the student and the examination-taking process
to ensure that the examination is completed without the aid of additional
persons or resources, unless approved by the Division; and protect the
confidentiality of educational materials. Proctors must be impartial third
parties and may not be an applicant's or licensee's sponsoring broker, managing
broker, relative, or colleague.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in;text-autospace:
none'>(Source:  Amended at 45 Ill. Reg. 2851, effective February 23, 2021)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>
<br><p class="subheading">SUBPART L:  CONTINUING EDUCATION TRANSITIONAL PROVISIONS</p>

<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-1.0in;text-autospace:
none'><b> </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-autospace:none'><b>Section 1450.1200  Continuing
Education Requirements for Transitioned Licensees (Repealed)</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>(Source: 
Repealed at 40 Ill. Reg. 12588, effective September 2, 2016)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-1.0in;text-autospace:
none'><b> </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.1205  Continuing Education Schools and
Courses (Repealed)</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in'>(Source:  Repealed at 45 Ill. Reg. 2851,
effective February 23, 2021)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.1210  Credit for Continuing Education
Courses (Repealed)</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in'>(Source:  Repealed at 45 Ill. Reg. 2851,
effective February 23, 2021)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.1215  Pre-license Schools and Courses
(Repealed)</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>(Source: 
Repealed at 45 Ill. Reg. 2851, effective February 23, 2021)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.1220  Education Provider, Course and
Instructor Transition (Repealed)</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>(Source: 
Repealed at 45 Ill. Reg. 2851, effective February 23, 2021)</p>

</div>

</body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-1.0in;text-autospace:
none'> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.1225  Core Continuing Education Course
Transition</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>a)         The
Division will accept applications for the broker 6-hour core continuing
education courses and the 12-hour managing broker continuing education course
that comply with the new requirements and curriculum established in this Part.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>b)         Effective
July 1, 2025, all broker and managing broker continuing education courses that
do not comply with the new requirements and curriculum approved by the Division
will not be accepted and are ineligible for renewal.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>c)         Applicants
for managing broker licenses that were up for renewal on April 30, 2025, are
not required to fulfill their 24-hour continuing education hours with courses
that include the updated requirements and curriculum.  However, applicants for
managing broker licenses that are up for renewal in the subsequent renewal
terms will be required to only take courses that include the updated core hours
and curriculum.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>d)         Applicants
for broker licenses that are up for renewal on April 30, 2026, are required to
fulfill their 12-hour continuing education hours with courses that include the
updated 6-hour core curriculum. However, if applicants for broker licenses take
and complete the previous 4-hour core curriculum between May 1, 2024, and June
30, 2025, they have fulfilled their core continuing education requirements and
will need 8 hours of elective continuing education for the 2026 broker renewal.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in'>(Source:  Added at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512,
effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>
<br><p class="subheading">SUBPART M:  REAL ESTATE AUCTION CERTIFICATION</p>

<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.1300  Real Estate Auction Pre-Certification
Education</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>a)         The
30-hour real estate certification course set forth in Section 5-32 of the Act
shall include the following: </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:.5in'>1)         18
hours relating to real estate including the following topics:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:.5in'>A)        Illinois
and federal statutes and rules governing real estate;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:.5in'>B)        Agency;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:.5in'>C)        Real
estate advertising; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>D)        Any
other subject matter recommended by the Division.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:.5in'>2)         12
hours relating to auctions including the following topics:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:.5in'>A)        Illinois
and federal statutes and rules governing auctions; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:.5in'>B)        Auction
advertising; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:2.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>C)        Any
other subject matter recommended by the Division.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>b)         The
30-hour real estate auction certification course may be completed in a
classroom, by interactive webinar, or by an online distance education course. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>c)         The Board
shall approve the 30-hour real estate auction course required by Section 25-110
of the Auction License Act [225 ILCS 407/25-110].  </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>(Source:  Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512,
effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.1310  Application for Real Estate Auction
Certification </b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal>To obtain a real estate auction certification, an applicant
must:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>a)         Submit
a signed and completed real estate auction certification application in a format
provided by the Division;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>b)         Hold a valid auctioneer
license under the Auction License Act [225 ILCS 407]; </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>c)         Pay the fee required by
Section 1450.130(d)(1); and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>d)         Complete
a 30-hour real estate auction certification course set forth in Section 1450.130,
including passing the mandatory proctored final course exam. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>(Source:  Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512,
effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>


<div>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal><b>Section 1450.1320  Real Estate Auction Certification
Activities</b></p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>a)         A
licensed auctioneer with a real estate auction certification, who is not
otherwise exempt from holding a broker or managing broker license under Section
5-20(13) of the Act, performing activities related to the auction of real
estate shall be limited to:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:.5in'>1)         Establishing
the time of the real estate auction;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:.5in'>2)         Establishing
the place of the real estate auction;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:.5in'>3)         Establishing
the method of the real estate auction;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>4)         Placing
proper advertisements regarding the real estate auction as set forth in
Sections 1450.715 and 1450.720; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:.5in'>5)         Crying
or calling the real estate auction.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>b)         A
licensed auctioneer exempt from holding a broker or managing broker license
under Section 5-20(13) of the Act who is performing activities related to the
auction of real estate shall be limited to:</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:.5in'>1)         Establishing
the time of the real estate auction;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:.5in'>2)         Establishing
the place of the real estate auction;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:.5in'>3)         Establishing
the method of the real estate auction;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>4)         Placing
proper advertisements regarding the real estate auction as set forth in
Sections 1450.715 and 1450.720;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:.5in'>5)         Crying
or calling the real estate auction; and</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.5in;text-indent:-.5in'>6)         Selling
or leasing real estate at auction.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:1.0in;text-indent:-.5in'>c)         As
set forth in Section 5-32 of the Act, the Department may revoke, suspend<span
class=msoIns><ins cite="mailto:Bockewitz,%20Crystal%20K."
datetime="2025-02-28T15:22">,</ins></span> or otherwise discipline the real
estate auction certification of a licensed auctioneer who violates Section 5-32
of the Act or Section 20-15 of the Auction License Act [225 ILCS 407].</p>

<p class=MsoNormal> </p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='text-indent:.5in'>(Source:  Amended at 49 Ill. Reg. 9512,
effective July 7, 2025)</p>

</div>

</td></tr></table></body>

</html>

		</text>
	</div>
</section>
